# Issues Priority List

Issues grouped by implementation tier. Work top-to-bottom within each tier; dependencies are noted where order matters within a tier. Ordering follows the sequencing in `PROJECT_BRIEF.md` §11, with one deliberate deviation: data safety (export/import) and a real deployment check are pulled up right after the first vertical slice, because IndexedDB is the only copy of the user's data and it's worth verifying the deployed build actually persists it before layering on more features — the same reasoning `life-kaleidoscope` used to pull its own backup issue forward.

---

## Tier 1 — Architecture foundation (Phase 1)
_Scaffolding, domain model, and persistence. Everything downstream depends on this. Ends with the Phase 1 architecture checkpoint._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#1](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/1) | ✅ Done | Epic 0 — Project scaffolding & tooling | |
| [#2](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/2) | ✅ Done | Epic 1 — Domain model & persistence layer | Depends on #1. Pause here for the Phase 1 checkpoint per `PROJECT_BRIEF.md` §11 |

_Shared primitives and routing skeleton before any real feature screen._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#3](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/3) | ✅ Done | Epic 2 — Design system & shared UI | Depends on Tier 1 |

## Tier 3 — First vertical slice
_Set a goal → log a day against it → real data exists. Goal setup alone produces nothing to look at; the slice isn't complete until a day can actually be logged._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#4](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/4) | ✅ Done | Epic 3 — Goal setup | Depends on Tier 2 |
| [#5](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/5) | ✅ Done | Epic 4 — Daily log entry | Depends on #4; this is the app's core daily-use loop |

---

## Tier 4 — Data safety & deployment (pulled forward)
_Once real daily entries exist (Tier 3), local-only IndexedDB is the only copy of that data — backup shouldn't wait until the end of the queue. Verifying the real deployed build persists data correctly is also worth doing early rather than discovering problems at the very end._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#9](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/9) | ✅ Done | Epic 8 — Export / Import | JSON export/import — the only backup mechanism since storage is local-only |
| [#10](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/10) | ✅ Done | Epic 9 — Deployment | GitHub Pages workflow; verify persistence survives a real deploy + reload |

---

## Tier 5 — UX & product-model rework (pulled forward)
_Live feedback on the deployed build surfaced a real bug and two direction changes that touch the shell and the goal model every later screen builds on. Resolving these before more feature screens land avoids building #6/#7/#8 against a shell and domain model that are about to change. Order matters within this tier: fix the input bug and redesign the shell first (self-contained), then decide the goal model (#14) before it blocks #6, then wire i18n through last so new copy doesn't need retranslating._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#12](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/12) | ✅ Done | Bug — decimal weight values can't be entered on mobile | Self-contained; blocks basic daily use on mobile |
| [#13](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/13) | ✅ Done | Redesign app shell — mobile-first bottom tab nav | Adopts the `life-kaleidoscope` shell pattern; should land before #6/#8 |
| [#14](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/14) | ✅ Done | Rework goal model — remove "big goal" framing, weekly-only goals | Decision: long-term target removed entirely, not just demoted. `Goal` is now weekly-pace-only |
| [#15](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/15) | ✅ Done | Add localization — English and Russian | `src/i18n/` (Dictionary + en/ru + useLocaleStore), wired through every screen and nav; switcher on /settings |
| [#16](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/16) | ✅ Done | Info tooltip on Calories field — day-lag with weight | New `InfoTooltip` shared primitive (tap-triggered Popover) + `tooltip?` prop on `NumberInput` |
| [#17](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/17) | ✅ Done | Appearance settings — mood (5: Pond/Dusk/Sage/Tortoise/Lagoon) + light/dark toggle | Final token map per `docs/design/color-palette-refinement.html`; new `--chart-weight`/`--chart-calories`/`--sand-foreground` tokens ready for #6 |

Mood (keeps swatch preview); sections wrapped in `Card`; `CardTitle` now a real `<h2>` |
| [#44](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/44) | ✅ Done | Add an overall mood-of-the-day, next to the daily note | New `DailyEntry.emotion?`, reuses existing `Emotion` type + `EmotionPicker`; bundled into the existing `saveNote()` action; added to export schema |
| [#45](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/45) | ✅ Done | Dashboard charts: tapping a point doesn't navigate on mobile | Root cause: Recharts' real click state has no `activePayload` field at all, so the resolver always returned null. Fixed to match `activeLabel` against the chart's own `data`; confirmed working via live feedback |
| [#49](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/49) | ✅ Done | Dashboard charts: tapping anywhere navigates instantly, chart unusable for just viewing | Custom `Tooltip` `content` with an in-tooltip link, container `onClick` dropped. Link initially didn't work: Recharts' tooltip wrapper is `pointer-events:none` by default — fixed via `wrapperStyle={{pointerEvents:'auto'}}`; confirmed on real device |
| [#46](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/46) | ✅ Done | Remove the weight projection/prognosis line from the dashboard chart | `goal` prop dropped from `WeightTrendChart`; `projectedPaceTrajectory` + test deleted outright, no other callers |
| [#47](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/47) | ✅ Done | History: From/To date filter inputs overlap on mobile | Third attempt: side-by-side with an explicit fixed `w-36` per input (not relative/shrink-based sizing) — confirmed on real device |
| [#48](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/48) | ✅ Done | History: add a calendar view alongside the current list view | List/Calendar `ToggleGroup` (#43); month grid with entry markers, tap a day for a read-only detail panel via new shared `DayDetail` (extracted from #39's EntryRow, reused by both); "Edit this day" reuses #40's From/To filter to jump to List pre-expanded. Also fixed a gap found in extraction: day mood (#44) wasn't shown in the read-only detail view at all |

## Tier 9 — Second live-feedback wave (2026-07-15)

_Same pattern as Tier 8: issues filed from continued live use. Ordered smaller-to-larger; #52/#53 depend on #51 landing first._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#56](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/56) | ✅ Done | Show the weekly target as a negative number (-0.5 kg), not unsigned | Negated the value passed to `formatNumber` in both `TodayScreen.tsx` and `GoalScreen.tsx`'s "This week's target" `StatCard`; `Goal.targetWeeklyLossKg` and the `GoalForm` input stay a positive magnitude, display-only change |
| [#57](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/57) | ✅ Done | Weight display rounds to 1 decimal instead of showing full entered precision | New `formatExactNumber()` (`minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 2`) in `i18n/formatNumber.ts`, applied to all 4 directly-entered/subtracted weight displays: `DailyEntryForm`'s read-only weight, `TodayScreen`'s vs-yesterday delta, `EntryRow`, `DayDetail`. Computed averages (weekly summaries, chart axes) untouched, still fixed 1-decimal |
| [#58](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/58) | ✅ Done | Add a README with a couple of screenshots | `README.md` at repo root; two real screenshots (`docs/screenshots/today.png`, `dashboard.png`) captured from the running app via seeded IndexedDB + `playwright screenshot --load-storage` |
| [#50](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/50) | ✅ Done | Reusable meal items — autocomplete meal names + editable library in Settings | New `domain/mealItem` + `IndexedDbMealItemRepository` (IndexedDB v2→v3, new `mealItems: 'id, &name'` table, no upgrade needed) + `useMealItemStore`. No foreign key from `CalorieEntry.note` — a `<datalist>` on both meal-note inputs suggests library names; adding/editing a meal with a note upserts it into the library by name (`touch`). Renaming a library item in Settings only affects future suggestions, never past entries; renaming onto an existing name merges into it |
| [#54](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/54) | ✅ Done | Meal emotions: replace happy/unhappy/neutral with thumbs-up/thumbs-down/bellissimo | Split `Emotion` (day, unchanged) from new `MealEmotion` (`thumbsUp`/`thumbsDown`/`bellissimo`); `EmotionPicker` generified over both sets. `bellissimo` renders as the 🤌 emoji (no lucide equivalent), not an icon. IndexedDB v3→v4 + export-bundle v3→v4 migrations clear old-format meal emotions outright (no auto-mapping), day emotion untouched |
| [#51](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/51) | ✅ Done | Add protein/fat/carbs per meal — capture fields + Today's per-day totals | Part 1 of 3. `CalorieEntry` gains optional `proteinG`/`fatG`/`carbsG` — purely additive, no IndexedDB or export-schema version bump needed. New `totalProtein`/`totalFat`/`totalCarbs` helpers (undefined, not 0, when nothing logged). Labeled kcal/protein/fat/carbs input row in both add and edit flows; per-meal and per-day macro summary lines, omitted (not "0g") when nothing logged that macro. Found and fixed a real pre-existing test-fragility bug along the way: `<datalist>` `<option>` text content could collide with real page text once a saved note became a library suggestion (#50) |
| [#52](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/52) | ✅ Done | Show protein/fat/carbs per-day totals in History | Part 2 of 3. New shared `macroDisplay.ts` (`formatMacroGrams`/`macrosSummaryText`, extracted from #51's `DailyEntryForm.tsx` for reuse) — day total shown under Calories in `EntryRow`'s table cell, plus per-meal and standalone-header day totals in `DayDetail` (calendar view). Confirmed working on real seeded data in-browser |
| [#53](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/53) | ✅ Done | Dashboard: protein/fat/carbs trend charts + weekly summary rows | Part 3 of 3. One combined `MacroTrendChart` (3 lines) rather than 3 separate charts — same unit (grams), comparable scale, keeps Dashboard from growing 3 more full-width charts. New `--chart-protein`/`--chart-fat`/`--chart-carbs` CSS tokens (constant across all 5 moods, light+dark variants, same pattern as `--chart-calories`) — confirmed legible in both light and dark via real screenshots. `weeklySummaries()` gained `averageProteinG`/`averageFatG`/`averageCarbsG`, shown in `WeeklySummaryCards`' description line |
| [#55](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/55) | ✅ Done | Celebrate reaching the weekly weight-loss target with a modal + "set new goal" CTA | New `shared/ui/dialog.tsx` (Radix Dialog wrapper, first modal primitive in the app). `useWeeklyGoalCelebration` hook fires as soon as the running `targetMet` crosses true, mid-week; re-fetches on every Today save so it's responsive within the same visit. New `goalCelebrationStore` persists only the most-recently-celebrated week's start — once celebrated, stays celebrated for that week even if the average dips back below target later (no flip-flopping). Fires independently of #38's end-of-week banner, confirmed via real screenshot |
| [#59](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/59) | ✅ Done | Add sleep tracking — duration + deep sleep | Capture + Today only; History/Dashboard display deferred. `DailyEntry` gains optional `sleepHours`/`deepSleepHours` — purely additive, no schema version bump. New "Sleep" section (after Weight, before Calories) follows the same read-only-with-pencil-to-edit pattern as Weight, both fields independently optional with a combined "Xh slept · Yh deep" summary (`—` per field not logged) |
| [#60](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/60) | ✅ Done | Add step count tracking | Capture + Today only; History/Dashboard display deferred. `DailyEntry.steps?` — purely additive, no schema version bump. New "Steps" section (after Sleep, before Calories), same read-only-with-pencil-to-edit pattern as Weight |
| [#61](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/61) | ✅ Done | Add an opt-in menstrual cycle tracker | Settings toggle (`cycleTrackingStore`, off by default, no gender field). `DailyEntry.onPeriod?: boolean` — purely additive, no schema version bump. Logging only, no phase/prediction computation. **Toggle's UI location moved off Today by #71** — see that row |
| [#65](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/65) | ✅ Done | Add a time-eaten field to meals, for intermittent fasting tracking | `CalorieEntry.timeEaten?: string` (`HH:MM`, purely additive, no schema bump). Native `<input type="time">` in both add and edit flows; defaults to current time only in the add flow (`currentTimeHHMM()`), re-defaults after each add. Editing an existing meal reflects its actual saved value, no forced default. Reordering meals does NOT clear time (decided against the original "reset on reorder" proposal — data-loss risk); confirmed via a dedicated drag-reorder test. Shown next to the meal ("Meal 1 — 420 kcal · 07:30") in both `DailyEntryForm` and `DayDetail`. No fasting-window calculation, capture only |
| [#64](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/64) | ✅ Done | Meal emotion icons look inconsistent — 2 monochrome lucide icons + 1 color emoji | `MEAL_EMOTIONS` (`emotionIcons.ts`) switched thumbsUp/thumbsDown from lucide `ThumbsUp`/`ThumbsDown` to 👍/👎 emoji, matching bellissimo's existing 🤌. `DAY_EMOTIONS` (happy/unhappy/neutral) untouched, still real lucide icons |
| [#63](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/63) | ✅ Done | Add a release notes section to Settings, updated per issue closure | New `src/data/releaseNotes.ts` (`{issue, date, en, ru}`, most-recent-first), backfilled for all 52 issues closed before this one; expandable `ReleaseNotesSection` in Settings (chevron toggle, same pattern as History's row expand). CLAUDE.md's issue-close rule now has a 3rd required step |
| [#62](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/62) | ✅ Done | Add a local food/nutrient database with quantity-based entry | Hand-curated `src/data/foods.ts` (~60 common foods, en+ru, per-100g kcal/protein/fat/carbs) rather than an external dataset, per scoping discussion. New "+ Food" button next to the manual add row opens `FoodPickerDialog` (Radix Dialog, #55's `shared/ui/dialog.tsx`) — search, pick, enter quantity in grams, macros scaled from the per-100g values. Adds a flat `CalorieEntry` (same shape manual entry produces, no new "meal group" model, per the earlier design note) |
| [#71](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/71) | ✅ Done | Cycle tracking toggle is visually noisy on Today — move to History's day view | Follow-up to #61, reported live: the toggle showed every day for something relevant ~5 days/month. Removed entirely from `DailyEntryForm`/Today; moved into `DayDetail.tsx` (shared by List's expanded row and Calendar's day panel) as a toggle next to that day's weight/calories, only visible when a specific day is opened. New optional `DayDetail.onSaved` prop threaded through `EntryRow`/`CalendarView`/`HistoryScreen`. `onPeriodLabel` copy changed from "On your period today" to "On period" since it no longer only applies to today |
| [#72](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/72) | ✅ Done | Calendar view: show a marker dot for days with the period logged | Follow-up to #71 — now that logging moved off Today, there was no at-a-glance view of which days had it. Second `size-1` dot in `CalendarView.tsx`'s day cells (`bg-destructive`, the mood-constant "danger" token, reused here purely for its color not its semantics), only rendered at all when cycle tracking is on so the grid doesn't reserve space for a marker most users never see |
| [#73](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/73) | ✅ Done | History table: trash icon still not visible after #67 | The #67 macro-compaction fix wasn't enough — the localized `'PP'` date format ("15 июл. 2026 г.") was still wide enough to push the Actions column's 3rd icon off screen. `EntryRow.tsx`'s Date cell switched to a fixed `dd.MM.yy` numeric format (locale-agnostic, no `dateFnsLocale` needed there anymore), freeing enough width for expand/edit/delete to all fit |
| [#74](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/74) | ✅ Done | Food picker dialog: list appears stuck at 6 items with no visible way to scroll | Root cause: a nested scroll region (`max-h-48` on the food `<ul>`) inside a `vh`-centered fixed dialog — on mobile, `vh` doesn't shrink when the keyboard opens, so part of the dialog (and most of the list's already-small scroll area) rendered behind the keyboard. `shared/ui/dialog.tsx`'s `DialogContent` now caps at `max-h-[85dvh]` (`dvh` tracks the real visible viewport) and scrolls as one unit; removed the food list's own nested scroll region |
| [#75](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/75) | ✅ Done | Food picker: show per-100g macros next to each food name | Small muted subtitle line under each food name in `FoodPickerDialog.tsx`'s list, reusing `macrosSummaryTextCompact` (#67) — lets you sanity-check a food's numbers before picking it rather than only after adding it as a meal |
| [#76](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/76) | ✅ Done | Remove unnecessary + sign from the kcal placeholder | `addCaloriesPlaceholder` simplified from `'+ kcal'`/`'+ ккал'` to `'kcal'`/`'ккал'` — you don't type a "+" into the field, and a separate "+ Food" affordance already exists nearby |
| [#77](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/77) | ✅ Done | Drop "(optional)" from the day Note field's label | `noteLabel` simplified from `'Note (optional)'`/`'Заметка (необязательно)'` to `'Note'`/`'Заметка'` — every field in the app is implicitly optional, so spelling it out on just this one label was inconsistent noise. The meal-note field's own placeholder (`mealNotePlaceholder`) is untouched |
| [#78](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/78) | ✅ Done | Expand the food list with data from two public sources | `foods.ts` grown from ~60 to 308 items, combining a RIA.ru calorie table (RU-cuisine) and a FitForFilms macros PDF (UK/international, raw weights, 100g rows only per instruction). Skipped near-duplicate entries, dried herbs/spices (not meaningfully trackable at 100g), and a niche probiotic-food section. Found and fixed a real perf regression along the way: `DailyEntryForm` rendered `FoodPickerDialog` unconditionally even while closed — cheap at ~60 items, measurably slow at 300+ under full-suite parallel test load. Now lazily mounted, only rendered while open |
| [#66](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/66) | ✅ Done | Move release notes from Settings to the About page | `ReleaseNotesSection.tsx` moved `features/settings/` → `features/about/`, same component/data/behavior. Dictionary keys deliberately left under `settings.*` (pure move, not a rename pass) |
| [#67](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/67) | ✅ Done | History table: macro summary overflow pushes expand/edit/delete icons off screen | New `macrosSummaryTextCompact()` in `macroDisplay.ts` — single-initial form ("P 20g · F 10g · C —" / "Б 20г · Ж 10г · У —"), used only in `EntryRow.tsx`'s Calories cell. `DailyEntryForm.tsx`/`DayDetail.tsx` keep the full-word `macrosSummaryText()`, unaffected |
| [#68](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/68) | ✅ Done | Steps input too wide, allows unrealistic values above 20,000/day | Input narrowed from `flex-1` to a fixed `w-24` (Sleep's fields are `w-20`; Steps needs 5 digits so slightly wider). `stepsSchema` max lowered from 100,000 to 20,000 |
| [#69](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/69) | ✅ Done | Sleep entry should allow hours + minutes, not decimal hours only | Split "Hours slept"/"Deep sleep" into hours+minutes integer sub-fields each (4 inputs total, per user's explicit choice over a single-field or one-field-split option). Storage stays decimal (`sleepHours`/`deepSleepHours`, unchanged) — `splitHoursMinutes`/`combineHoursMinutes` convert only at the UI boundary. Read-only summary switched from "7.5h slept" to "7h 30m slept" to match. `deepSleepHoursLabel` renamed to `deepSleepLabel` ("Deep sleep (hours)" → "Deep sleep", now a group label for both sub-fields) |

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |

| [#79](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/79) | ✅ Done | Manual meal "Add" button placement is confusing | Add and + Food both moved after the note/mood row; Add is now full-width `variant="default"` (was `outline`, part of the fields row above the note) |
| [#80](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/80) | ✅ Done | Meal note placeholder is confusing with the daily note | `mealNotePlaceholder` reworded from "Note (optional)"/"Заметка (необязательно)" to "Add a dish?"/"Добавить своё блюдо?" — used by both the add-meal and edit-meal note inputs |
| [#81](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/81) | ✅ Done | Group multiple food items under one meal | `CalorieEntry` restructured: `items: CalorieItem[]` (name+kcal+macros each), `note`/`emotion`/`timeEaten` stay group-level. New `calorieEntryKcal`/`calorieEntryProtein/Fat/Carbs` helpers; `totalCalories`/`totalMacros` updated to sum via items. IndexedDB v5 + export-bundle v5 migrations fold existing flat meals into single-item groups. Flexible grouping (no fixed Breakfast/Lunch/Dinner slots) — the bottom Add row always starts a new group; adding another item to an *existing* group happens via its Edit mode ("+ Add item"), which also supports per-item delete. Copy split: item name placeholder "Add a dish?" (moved from #80's meal-note copy), group note repurposed to "Anything else about this meal?" |
| [#82](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/82) | ✅ Done | Time-eaten field should not be pre-populated | Reverses #65's "defaults to now" decision. Add-flow Time input now starts empty (`useState('')`) and resets to empty after each add, same as the other add-* fields. Edit-meal flow unaffected |
| [#83](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/83) | ✅ Done | Food list is missing prepared/composite dishes (jam, stew, sandwich, pizza, pasta) | 34 new entries added (308 → 342): 26 EN/international dishes sourced from USDA FNDDS (pizza, burgers, pasta, sushi, curries, etc.), 8 RU-cuisine dishes from `health-diet.ru` (beef stroganoff, azu, vareniki, etc.). Same shape as existing entries, no schema change; de-duplicated against the existing 308 items first |
| [#84](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/84) | ✅ Done | Selected emotion icon not visible enough in dark mode | `EmotionPicker`'s selected state changed from `bg-muted text-foreground` to `border-2 border-primary bg-primary/15` — `--primary` is reliably high-contrast against background in every mood theme. Fixes both the meal-emotion and day-mood pickers |
| [#85](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/85) | ✅ Done | Add a week-start preference (Monday vs the day I started tracking) | New `weekStartStore` (`'monday' \| 'firstEntryWeekday'`, default `'monday'`), Settings toggle "Monday"/"Day of my first entry". `currentWeekInfo.ts`/`weeklySummaries.ts` switched from hardcoded `startOfISOWeek`/`endOfISOWeek` to `startOfWeek`/`endOfWeek` + an explicit `weekStartsOn` param; new `resolveWeekStartsOn()` (`shared/lib/`) and `useWeekStartsOn()` (`shared/hooks/`) translate the stored preference for each call site (`useCurrentWeekInfo`, `WeeklySummaryCards`, `MetTargetList`, `CorrelationView`, `useWeeklyGoalCelebration`) |
| [#86](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/86) | ✅ Done | Can't reliably find or reuse a previously-added meal item | `MealItem` gains optional `lastAmountKcal`/`lastProteinG`/`lastFatG`/`lastCarbsG` (purely additive), updated by `touch()` on every save. Native `<datalist>` replaced with `MealNoteAutocomplete.tsx` (hand-rolled dropdown, reliable on iOS). `FoodPickerDialog` search merges `foods.ts` + `mealItems` via a `PickableItem` union — personal items reuse last-logged absolute numbers (no quantity scaling), curated foods unchanged |
| [#87](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/87) | ✅ Done | Rename the daily note field to disambiguate from meal notes | `noteLabel` renamed "Note"/"Заметка" → "Day's note"/"Заметка дня"; new `noteFieldPlaceholder` ("Want to share anything for the day?"/"Как прошёл день?") added to the editable input, which previously had none |
| [#88](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/88) | ✅ Done | Dashboard chart tap-to-navigate goes to the wrong day | Root cause: the in-tooltip nav link (#49, `pointerEvents:'auto'`) renders inside Recharts' own move-tracking wrapper, so a finger drifting toward the link could bubble a touchmove up and silently retarget the "active" point to a neighboring date. Fixed with `onMouseMove`/`onTouchMove` + `stopPropagation()` on the tooltip's own div in `WeightTrendChart.tsx`/`CalorieTrendChart.tsx`/`MacroTrendChart.tsx`. Reasoned fix from symptom + screenshots, not independently verified on-device (same caveat as #74) |
| [#89](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/89) | ✅ Done | Collapse the correlation chart until there's enough data for an insight | `CorrelationView.tsx`'s scatter plot collapses by default (chevron toggle, same pattern as About's `ReleaseNotesSection`) until `correlationInsight()` has 4+ comparable weeks — the near-empty plot read as broken for the first several weeks of use. Caveat text stays visible either way; only the chart itself is opt-in |
| [#90](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/90) | ✅ Done | Let users customize the curated food list (hide/edit items) | New `domain/foodOverride` + IndexedDB v6 (`foodOverrides: '&foodId'`, new store, no upgrade needed) + `useFoodOverrideStore`. Pure `applyFoodOverrides()`/`effectiveFoodItem()` helpers merge overrides into `foods.ts` at read time — used by `FoodPickerDialog` (hides/corrects search results) and the new `/settings/foods` screen (`FoodListSettingsScreen.tsx`, reached via a new Settings card — its own route since 300+ items don't fit inline). Local-only, not exported (same precedent as `mealItems`) |

## Tier 13 — Sixth live-feedback wave (2026-07-17)

_Same pattern as Tiers 8–12: issues filed from continued live use._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#100](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/100) | ✅ Done | Today screen: add a stat card for progress vs. maximum recorded weight | New `useMaxRecordedWeight(refreshKey)` hook + third `StatCard`, same asymmetric bold/muted treatment as #42's vs-yesterday card. Found and fixed a real test-collision bug along the way: two existing "delta vs yesterday" tests coincidentally produced the same delta text as the new card and needed scoping to their own `[data-slot="card"]` |
| [#101](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/101) | ✅ Done | Move Steps field to the bottom of the daily entry form, near the day note | Pure reordering, no state/logic changes — Steps block relocated to sit right before the Day note. Full suite (457/457) unaffected |
| [#102](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/102) | ✅ Done | Blank screen when opening the app from the iOS home-screen shortcut | Shipped: `errorElement: <RouteErrorFallback />` on the top-level route (friendly reload prompt instead of a silent blank unmount), a pure-CSS `#root:empty::before` spinner in `index.html`, route-based code splitting (main chunk 1.3MB → ~480KB gzipped-147KB, a real independent win). **Actual root cause, found live**: `public/manifest.json`'s `start_url`/icon `src` were absolute-root paths (`"/"`) — manifest JSON content is copied verbatim by Vite, never rewritten with the `--base=/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/` deploy prefix the way `index.html`'s own `<link>`/`<script>` tags are. iOS's "Add to Home Screen" resolved `start_url: "/"` against the bare origin `https://zhannam85.github.io/`, not the actual project-site subpath — confirmed directly by inspecting a reporter's "Add to Home Screen" dialog. Explains both the original slow/blank report and a fresh live 404 repro. Fixed with spec-relative manifest paths (`"start_url": "./"`, `"scope": "./"`, bare-relative icon `src`), which resolve correctly relative to the manifest's own location under any base path. **Any existing home-screen shortcut still points at the wrong URL and must be removed and re-added** — the fix only affects new shortcuts going forward |
| [#115](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/115) | ✅ Done | Detect and prompt about new app versions (no refresh/update mechanism today) | `vite.config.ts` bakes `__APP_VERSION__` (the CI commit SHA) into the bundle at build time; `deploy-pages.yml` writes the same SHA to `public/version.json` before building. New `useAppUpdateAvailable` hook polls it every 5 min; `AppUpdateBanner.tsx` shows a Reload prompt when they differ. Simple polling, no service worker, as scoped |
| [#116](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/116) | ✅ Done | Add a chart correlating latest meal time with next-day weight | New `domain/stats/lateMealCorrelation.ts` (`lateMealPoints`/`lateMealCorrelation`, `MIN_COMPARABLE_DAYS = 8`) + `LateMealCorrelationView.tsx` on the Dashboard, mirroring `CorrelationView`/`correlationInsight`'s shape and #89's collapsed-until-real-data pattern. Own copy/caveat, distinct from the weekly calories-vs-weight correlation |
| [#117](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/117) | ✅ Done | Time field can't be reliably cleared once tapped (native iOS picker Reset doesn't work) | App-level `X` clear button added next to both Time fields (add row + item-edit row), shown only when the field has a value, calling `setAddTime('')`/`onEditTimeChange('')` directly — bypasses the native picker's Reset entirely |
| [#103](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/103) | ✅ Done | Home-screen icon looks broken — generic gray tile with a truncated name | `apple-touch-icon`/`apple-mobile-web-app-title`/`apple-mobile-web-app-capable`/`manifest.json`/`theme-color` wired into `index.html`. Icon artwork initially a hand-rolled geometric mark, later swapped for a proper illustrated turtle graphic the user provided — resized into all sizes with `jimp` (temporary dev-only install, `--no-save`, removed after use). `favicon.svg` replaced with a plain PNG favicon (`favicon-64.png`) since the new artwork is a raster illustration |
| [#104](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/104) | ✅ Done | Calendar view: the entry/period dots stack vertically, should sit side by side | Wrapped both dot `<span>`s in a `flex flex-row items-center gap-0.5` sibling of the day number instead of leaving them as direct `flex-col` children. Existing tests query dots via `span[aria-hidden="true"]`, unaffected by the new non-hidden wrapper |
| [#105](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/105) | ✅ Done | Russian meal-note placeholder reads too formal/official | `ru.ts`'s `mealNotePlaceholder` ("Что-нибудь ещё об этом приёме пищи?") → "Было вкусно?", per user's pick between two options. RU-only copy change, English untouched |
| [#106](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/106) | ✅ Done | Manual meal entry: move "Find food" directly above the Add button, with an "or" divider | New `orDivider` i18n key ("or"/"или") rendered between the note/mood row and "Find food", which now sits above Add. Purely positional + one new string, no state changes |
| [#107](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/107) | ✅ Done | Manual meal entry: move Time up to the meal heading row, keep the fields row macros-only | Bottom add row's heading is now `flex justify-between` with Time right-aligned; fields row is macros-only. Item-edit row's Time was already separate/group-level, out of scope. Related: #106 |
| [#108](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/108) | ✅ Done | Russian item-name placeholder reads as a yes/no question | `ru.ts`'s `itemNamePlaceholder` ("Добавить своё блюдо?") → "Создать блюдо?". RU-only copy change, same pattern as #105 |
| [#109](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/109) | ✅ Done | Manual add row: disable the Add button until there's a valid amount to add | `disabled={!addKcal100Preview \|\| addKcal100Preview <= 0}` on the Add button, reusing the already-computed `addKcal100Preview` (#98) rather than a second parse. The item-edit row's Save was checked too — it's not a matching gap, saving with all items cleared is documented intentional "delete this meal" behavior (see #81 in ARCHITECTURE.md), left as-is |
| [#110](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/110) | ✅ Done | Let users customize meal names (Breakfast/Lunch/Dinner) instead of always Meal 1/2/3 | New optional `CalorieEntry.label` (additive, no version bump) + edit-mode Input replacing the static heading in `MealListItem`, defaulting to `mealLabel(n)`. New `useMealLabelPresetStore` (localStorage, like `weekStartStore`) + `MealLabelPresetsSection.tsx` in Settings for managing quick-pick names, offered as chips in the edit-mode label input. Built-in defaults (Breakfast/Lunch/Dinner/Snack, translated) are one-click adds, not auto-seeded, so a later language switch doesn't strand stale-language presets |

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#122](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/122) | ✅ Done | Add/edit meal-item fields (kcal/protein/fat/carbs/grams) are tiny and hard to operate on mobile | New `MealItemEditorSheet.tsx` — a full-screen `Dialog` (new `size="fullscreen"` on `dialog.tsx`) with `h-11`/`text-base` fields, replacing the cramped `flex flex-wrap` row of `h-7 w-16` inputs everywhere it appeared. Existing meal items now show as a compact one-line summary (name — kcal · macros) with Pencil (opens the sheet)/Trash2, instead of always-expanded fields. The primary "add a new meal" row also converts to a "+ Add item" tap-to-open trigger — the reporter confirmed this tradeoff (one extra tap vs. always-visible cramped fields) explicitly. Purely a UI/layout change: no data-model or persistence changes, same `addMeal()`/`saveEditMeal()` commit paths, same `macroScaling.ts` math |
| [#123](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/123) | ✅ Done | Add an Excel (.xlsx) export option alongside the JSON backup | New "Export as Excel" button next to the existing JSON one, `exportXlsx.ts`'s `buildExportWorkbook()` (three sheets: Daily Log, Meals, Goals — raw numeric cells + real Date cells, not locale-formatted strings). Distinct filename from the JSON backup so it doesn't read as re-importable (it isn't). Dependency choice: the standard `xlsx`/SheetJS npm package has unpatched high-severity CVEs with no fix on the npm registry — user chose `exceljs` instead (clean audit, standard install) over sourcing `xlsx` from SheetJS's own CDN. `exceljs` is dynamically imported only when the button is clicked (~930KB/256KB gzipped its own chunk), so it doesn't affect the Settings route's normal load |
| [#124](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/124) | ✅ Done | Find food is only available on the new-meal row, not when editing an existing meal | Pre-existing gap (not introduced by #122), just more noticeable once manual entry became a dedicated sheet. Each `MealListItem` now has its own local "Find food" trigger + `FoodPickerDialog` instance, right after "+ Add item" — a picked food converts to a per-100g rate via `ratesFromAbsolute` and lands in that meal's `editItems` staging array, same "restore a suggestion" convention `selectEditItemMealItem` already used |
| [#125](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/125) | ✅ Done | Add a CSV export option, better suited for feeding to an LLM | New "Export as CSV" button, `exportCsv.ts`'s hand-written `buildDailyLogCsv()` (RFC 4180 quoting, no library needed — unlike the Excel export). Single flat Daily Log table (same shape as the Excel export's first sheet), no Meals/Goals sheets — kept to one file for a simple download UX. `InfoTooltip` next to the button explains CSV is the best format to paste into an LLM. UTF-8 BOM prefix on the downloaded file so Cyrillic notes don't mangle if opened in Excel instead |

## Tier 17 — Spacing & sizing consistency, meal reactions (2026-07-17)
_Live-use feedback flagged two visual-consistency bugs (#126, #127) and a feature request for per-food reactions (#129) that #128's "most liked/disliked foods" list depends on for accurate attribution._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#126](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/126) | ✅ Done | Unify heights of standalone form fields and buttons (Date, Weight, Sleep, Steps, meal item editor) | Today's Date input and Weight/Sleep/Steps/Note's display rows + edit inputs are all `h-11` (44px), matching `MealItemEditorSheet`'s existing field height. New `icon-xl` (`size-11`) `Button` size for their adjacent pencil/Save buttons. Dense inline list-row controls in meal editing intentionally left smaller (out of scope) |
| [#127](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/127) | ✅ Done | Interactive element clusters too tightly packed — need min 12px horizontal gap | Bumped `gap-1`/`gap-1.5`/`gap-2` (4–8px) to `gap-3` (12px) across `DailyEntryForm.tsx`'s interactive-element clusters (meal-name Save/Delete, item-row Pencil/Delete, reaction picker, time-clear button, label-preset chips, view-mode Pencil/Delete, day-mood picker) and `MealItemEditorSheet.tsx`'s 100g/Portion toggle. Left icon-prefix-to-text and value-to-unit-label pairings (drag handle, Clock icon, InfoTooltip, sleep hours/minutes sub-fields) alone — those read as one visual unit, not a crowded control cluster |
| [#129](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/129) | ✅ Done | Move meal reaction (emotion) from meal group to individual food | `CalorieItem` gains `emotion?: MealEmotion`, removed from `CalorieEntry` entirely. `EmotionPicker` extracted to its own file (avoids a circular import with `MealItemEditorSheet.tsx`, which now hosts the per-item picker). `DayDetail.tsx`'s read view and the Excel export's "Meals" sheet updated to per-item reactions too. Export bundle schema bumped to v6 (`ExportBundleV5` frozen alongside it); IndexedDB bumped to Dexie v7 — both fold a single-item meal's old group reaction onto its item, drop it (ambiguous) for a multi-item meal. Prerequisite for #128's precise attribution |
| [#128](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/128) | ✅ Done | Feature request: most liked/disliked foods list based on meal reactions | New `domain/stats/foodReactions.ts` (`mostLikedFoods`/`mostDislikedFoods`, precise per-item attribution now that #129 landed) + new Dashboard section `FoodReactionsView.tsx` — two ranked lists, top 5 each, bellissimo-weighted scoring for "liked." Renders nothing until at least one item has a logged reaction |
| [#130](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/130) | ✅ Done | Increase vertical spacing between stacked elements in the meal list (mobile) | Bumped the meal-groups list (between "Обед"/"Перекус"/etc. blocks) from `gap-1` to `gap-3` (12px, the biggest jump — this was the main "running into each other" complaint), the item-drafts list in edit mode from `gap-1` to `gap-2`, the view-mode meal row's internal stack from `gap-0.5` to `gap-1.5`, and the item sub-list from `gap-0.5` to `gap-1` — smaller bumps for the more tightly-related nested content |
| [#131](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/131) | ✅ Done | ARCHITECTURE.md per-component rows have grown into unreadable single-line diffs | `DailyEntryForm.tsx`'s 19,497-char single-line row split into 8 consecutive same-table rows (longest line dropped to 3,304 chars), via a script that verified an exact content round-trip before writing — no prose lost or altered. Added a convention note above the `daily-log/` table: new notes on an already-large row should become a new row, not be appended to the existing line. No release-notes.ts entry — pure internal docs change, no user-facing behavior |
| [#132](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/132) | ✅ Done | Feature request: customizable Dashboard chart with toggleable data series | New `domain/stats/customChartSeries.ts` + `CustomChartView.tsx`. Design decisions confirmed with the user before building: mixed-scale series (kg/kcal/g/steps) normalized to 0-100 per series on one shared axis rather than multiple axes; period/bowel-movement originally rendered as vertical `ReferenceLine` markers, replaced by dots in #137 |
| [#133](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/133) | ✅ Done | Meal add-row controls (time, note, +Add item, reaction) still smaller than standalone fields | Add-row and existing-meal edit-row Time/clear-button/"+ Add item"/note all moved to `h-11`/`icon-xl`. `EmotionPicker` gained an optional `size` prop (default `icon-sm`, unchanged for day-mood) so the reaction picker — now living inside `MealItemEditorSheet.tsx` since #129, not this row — could also move to `icon-xl` |
| [#134](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/134) | ✅ Done | Foods added via "Find food" have no way to set a reaction | `FoodPickerDialog.tsx`'s `onAdd` now carries an optional `emotion`, settable via an `EmotionPicker` in the sticky footer once a food is picked (either source). Both `DailyEntryForm.tsx` call sites set it on the created item/draft. User-discovered gap in #129, filed and fixed same session |
| [#137](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/137) | ✅ Done | Compare your data: pick a chart type (line/bar/dots) per series, dots for period/bowel-movement markers | `CustomChartView.tsx` switched from `LineChart` to `ComposedChart`; each selected numeric series gets its own line/bar/dots `ToggleGroup` (icon buttons) in the legend, defaulting to line. "Dots" for both numeric series and the period/bowel-movement markers reuse a `Line` with a transparent stroke and a visible `dot` (not `Scatter`) so everything shares the existing category x-axis with no new axis wiring. Boolean flags always render as a dot pinned to the top of the chart (`BOOLEAN_MARKER_Y`), replacing the old full-height `ReferenceLine` per flagged day |
| [#139](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/139) | ✅ Done | Native date/time inputs render taller than other standalone fields on mobile Safari | User-confirmed direction (reversed from the initial framing): keep the native date/time controls' own rendered size, grow everything else to match. `h-11`/`icon-xl` (`size-11`) bumped to `h-12`/`size-12` across `DailyEntryForm.tsx`, `TodayScreen.tsx`, `MealItemEditorSheet.tsx`, and the `icon-xl` `Button` size definition itself. No exact on-device measurement available (no iOS simulator access) — `h-12` is a reasonable first step, pending the user's visual confirmation on a real phone |
| [#136](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/136) | ✅ Done | History: editing a day causes horizontal scroll that cuts off the meal-edit form | `EntryRow.tsx`'s edit-mode `<td colSpan={5}>` (wrapping `DailyEntryForm`) gained `block` — a `table-layout: auto` `<td>` sizes to its content's intrinsic width regardless of `max-w-full`/`min-w-0`, so the full-width `DailyEntryForm` was forcing the whole table wider than the viewport. `display: block` opts that one cell out of table layout entirely, so it behaves like a normal block box |
| [#138](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/138) | ✅ Done | Today: add left/right arrows to navigate to the previous/next day | `TodayScreen.tsx` gained `ChevronLeft`/`ChevronRight` `icon-xl` buttons flanking the Date input, stepping `date` state by one day via `date-fns addDays`. Resolved the open design question conservatively: right arrow disabled once already on today (matching the date input's existing `max` cap), no future-day logging added — that's a separate, bigger feature not requested here |
| [#143](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/143) | ✅ Done | Give each meal group a card-style background/border, matching stat cards | Every `MealListItem` state (view/editing/confirm-delete) and the add-row container now use `rounded-xl bg-card p-3 ring-1 ring-foreground/10`, the same classes `shared/ui/card.tsx`'s `Card` uses for "This week's target"/"vs. yesterday". Add-row's old top-border divider dropped in favor of the card's own boundary. Verified with a seeded Playwright screenshot |
| [#141](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/141) | ✅ Done | Default meal headings to Breakfast/Lunch/Dinner/Snack by position instead of Meal 1/2/3 | New `shared/lib/mealLabel.ts`: `defaultMealLabel(t, position)` (the four translated presets by index, falling back to positional "Meal N" from the 5th meal on) and `effectiveMealLabel(t, position, label)` (custom label wins when set). Used by `DailyEntryForm.tsx`'s heading/add-row preview, `DayDetail.tsx` (which — pre-existing gap found while implementing this — never read `meal.label` at all, always showed "Meal N" even with a custom label set), and `exportXlsx.ts`'s Meals sheet. Aria-label disambiguation suffixes (internal a11y anchors, not user-facing) deliberately kept positional |
| [#146](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/146) | ✅ Done | Meal-name edit's save checkmark isn't clear enough — users think it didn't save | Save button changed from `variant="ghost"` (blended into the background) to `variant="outline"` (visible border) — Delete stays `ghost` since it's secondary/destructive. Also added the missing Enter-to-save `onKeyDown` on the name input, matching every other field in `DailyEntryForm.tsx` |
| [#142](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/142) | ✅ Done | Settings: meal-name preset suggestions duplicate across English/Russian | `MealLabelPresetsSection.tsx`'s `unaddedDefaults` now checks a preset against *every* locale's translation (`getDictionary(locale).dailyEntry.defaultMealNamePresets[index]` for each `Locale`), not just the active one — the four defaults are positionally aligned across `en.ts`/`ru.ts`, so index 0 is always "Breakfast"/"Завтрак" together |
| [#135](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/135) | ✅ Done | This week's target isn't anchored to when you set it, so target periods vary in length | `Goal` gained `weekStart?: string`, stamped fresh to today by `formValuesToGoal` on every save — a real 7-day window (`weekStart`..`goalWeekEnd(weekStart)`, new `domain/goal/goalWindowProgress.ts`) instead of a fixed calendar grid. `TodayScreen`/`GoalScreen`'s target `StatCard` show that range; #38's renewal banner now fires once the window is complete (and stays showing until renewed, not just its exact last day); #55's celebration modal reads `goalWindowProgress()` instead of `weeklySummaries()`'s calendar-week grouping. `weeklySummaries.ts`/`useWeekStartsOn` untouched — still calendar-grid-based for Dashboard/History's retrospective views, a separate concern. #18's `currentWeekInfo.ts`/`useCurrentWeekInfo` removed as dead code (their only consumer was the StatCard this replaced); `DailyEntryRepository.getEarliestDate()` removed too (only caller was that hook) |
| [#144](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/144) | ✅ Done | Meal edit-name state breaks the layout | Confirmed via a seeded Playwright screenshot (clicked the pencil on a real meal, screenshotted the resulting `isEditing` state) that #143's card treatment already resolves this — it wraps the *entire* edit state (name input, preset chips, item list, "+ Add item"/"Find food", Time, Note), not just the name row, in one `rounded-xl bg-card p-3 ring-1 ring-foreground/10` boundary. No additional code change needed; closed as already-fixed by #143 |
| [#140](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/140) | ✅ Done | Meal item editor: enter count of 100g portions instead of total grams | The per-100g quantity field now takes a portion count ("2" = 200g, "1.5" = 150g) instead of the raw gram total, matching how nutrition labels are printed. `scaleFromPer100g`/`ratesFromAbsolute` (`shared/lib/macroScaling.ts`) reframed around portions (blank/invalid now defaults to 1 portion instead of 100g); new `portionsToGrams()` helper for the two per-100g/per-portion mode-switch conversions that still need true grams. `amountG` stays real grams everywhere else (export, `CalorieItem`, `MealItem`) — only the input field's unit and label ("× 100g"/"× 100 г", renamed from "Grams") changed |
| [#147](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/147) | ✅ Done | Show a history of past weekly targets, not just the current one | Design decision: every goal save now creates a fresh historical `Goal` record (`formValuesToGoal` always mints a new `id`/`createdAt`, never reuses `existingGoal`'s) instead of overwriting the previous one — `GoalRepository.getActiveGoal()`/`getAll()` needed zero changes, since "most recent by createdAt" and "everything" both already meant what they now provide. New `domain/goal/goalHistory.ts`'s `pastGoals()` (every non-active goal, newest first, each paired with its own `goalWindowProgress()`) + `usePastGoals` hook + `PastTargetsList.tsx`, rendered on `GoalScreen` below the form — same plain-record visual language as History's `MetTargetList`, no badges. "How long it took" deliberately kept simple: each target's own fixed 7-day window (`weekStart`–`weekEnd`), not a finer "days until pace was met" calculation |
| [#145](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/145) | ✅ Done | No focused single-meal editor — editing a meal always pulls in the whole day's form | User picked the additive option over replacing History's day-edit entirely (a second, bigger option) or closing as out of scope. Extracted the meal-group list + add row verbatim (no behavior change) from `DailyEntryForm.tsx` into standalone `MealList.tsx` (`{ calorieEntries, onChange }`) — Today mounts it exactly as before. `DayDetail.tsx` (History's read-only expand-row, shared by `EntryRow.tsx`'s list view and `CalendarView.tsx`'s day panel) now also mounts it whenever its `onSaved` prop is present, so meals are directly editable from History without ever opening `EntryRow`'s full `alwaysEditable` form (Weight/Sleep/Steps/Note untouched). `DayDetail` without `onSaved` keeps the original read-only rendering |

## Tier 18 — Ninth live-feedback wave (2026-07-19)
_Same pattern as Tiers 8–17: issues filed from continued live use. Ordered easiest → hardest per the user's explicit request; #155 depends on #154 landing first, and #155 itself has an open design question flagged in its own issue body._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#151](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/151) | ✅ Done | Add a clear (X) button to the meal-name input | New `resetItemDraft()` in `MealList.tsx` (shared with `addMeal()`'s existing post-save reset) + a `ghost`/`icon-xl` `X` button next to the "+ Add item" trigger, shown once a staged draft has a valid amount |
| [#150](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/150) | ✅ Done | Bug: standard catalog foods leak into the personal meal dictionary via meal-edit save | New module-level `curatedFoodNames` `Set` (every `foods.ts` entry's en+ru name) in `MealList.tsx`, gating `saveEditMeal()`'s touch loop — a name matching a curated food is skipped, only free-typed names get upserted. Regression test added in `DailyEntryForm.test.tsx` covering the exact repro (find-food into an existing meal, save, assert `db.mealItems` stays empty) |
| [#149](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/149) | ✅ Done | Add a way to create a custom dictionary entry from Settings, not just via logging a meal | New `AddMealItemForm` in `MealItemsSection.tsx` (same name + per-100g fields as the existing row editor, blank draft) behind an "Add custom food" button, calling the existing `touch(name, nutrition)` upsert directly — no schema/repository change |
| [#152](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/152) | ✅ Done | Give the day's total macros their own field on Today, not a caption line | Promoted `dayMacrosSummary` from a `text-xs` caption line to its own labeled field (`text-sm font-medium` label + `bg-muted` box), matching Calories/Weight/Sleep's existing treatment |
| [#153](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/153) | ✅ Done | Reorder/resize the add-item CTAs: Find food first and primary, + Add item as fallback link | Applied to both the bottom add row and the existing-meal edit row's "+ Add item"/"Find food" pair for consistency. Note field moved below both CTAs on the add row |
| [#154](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/154) | ✅ Done | Bug: past-targets goal history shows nothing after a confirmed goal replacement | Reported before #181 existed, when *every* save minted a fresh `Goal` record — including a same-week resubmit, which is exactly the reported repro (reach goal, save again, expect a 2nd past-targets row). #181 changed that: a save while the window is still live now edits the same record in place (`isEditingLiveWindow`), so that specific duplicate-same-week-record path no longer exists at all. Verified live with a seeded two-goal scenario (an ended-window goal + a current one, genuinely different `weekStart`s, matching how a real replacement looks under #181's model) — Past Targets renders the older goal correctly. No additional code change needed; closed as already-fixed by #181/#182 |
| [#155](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/155) | ✅ Done | Show when a weekly goal was reached on History (List + Calendar) | Implemented per the decision worked through with the reporter: (1) `isEditingLiveWindow` (#181) gains an `activeGoalReached` condition — once `goalWindowProgress().metOnDate` is set, the next save starts a fresh record instead of editing the succeeded one in place; `GoalForm`'s new `activeGoalReached` prop (from `GoalScreen`'s own `useActiveGoalProgress`) supplies it. (2) `GoalScreen` shows a "Target met on [date]" badge alongside the target StatCard's week-range description plus a quiet nudge banner (#38 tone/style) once reached. (3) New `domain/goal/reachedGoalWindows.ts` (`reachedGoalWindows`/`isDateWithinReachedWindow`/`isGoalMetOnDate`) computes every `[weekStart, metOnDate]` span across all goals; `useHistoryData` now also loads every goal (not just the active one) to feed it. `EntryRow.tsx`'s date cell and `CalendarView.tsx`'s day cells get a background tint for days within a reached window, a stronger tint for the exact reach day, plus sr-only/aria-label text for accessibility — `useActiveGoalProgress` extracted from `useWeeklyGoalCelebration` so both screens share the same live-refreshing progress data |

## Tier 19 — Tenth live-feedback wave (2026-07-19)
_Filed mid-Tier-18 from continued live use, once #149-151 had already shipped. Per explicit instruction, filed without pre-implementation investigation — root causes below are still open. Ordered into the existing Tier 18 queue: #152, #158, #153, #156, #154, #155, #157 (#157 last — it's the biggest and most disruptive, potentially reversing #145's prior explicit "inline, not a separate view" decision, and is flagged as a genuine design fork to confirm before building, not a routine auto-proceed item)._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#158](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/158) | ✅ Done | Revisit #146: meal edit still shows a small checkmark icon, not a big Save button | Replaced the small header checkmark with a full-width, text-labeled `size="lg"` Save button at the bottom of the edit card (same prominence as `MealItemEditorSheet`'s own Save) |
| [#156](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/156) | ✅ Done | #144 still reproducing: empty space on the right of view-mode meal cards | Longest-running fix of this batch — full history worth keeping for next time this class of bug shows up. Two wrong closes first: (1) not-a-bug from a desktop-Chromium measurement, reopened after the reporter confirmed it live on Safari; (2) fixed #152's Macros box (real, kept) but didn't fully explain it. Reporter's own correlations found two more real bugs: (3) long dish names — a `flex-1 truncate` span with no `min-w-0` (flex items default to `min-width: auto`, so `truncate` never engaged for long text), fixed with `min-w-0`; (4) the reaction emoji specifically — `text-sm leading-none` on the emoji span forced an inconsistent line-box inside wrapping text, WebKit rendering it as visible overlap, fixed by dropping `leading-none` (3 places: `MealList.tsx` x2, `DayDetail.tsx`). Neither fully explained the remaining reports, so switched to **live debug instrumentation** (temporary bright outlines + a `getBoundingClientRect()`/`window.innerWidth` badge, shipped and reverted in throwaway commits) instead of guessing from screenshots — found the real final cause: with a text input focused (keyboard open) in **standalone PWA mode specifically** (not a plain Safari tab), `window.innerWidth`/`scrollWidth` would balloon (393→483 on the reporter's device) while every actual element correctly stayed at the true screen width — a known WebKit viewport-misreporting quirk. Fixed with `interactive-widget=resizes-content` on the viewport meta (`index.html`, iOS 17.4+). Verified live via the debug badge before/after on the reporter's own device |
| [#157](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/157) | ✅ Done | Consider a dedicated view for editing a meal, instead of inline expansion on the same page | New route `/entry/:date/meal/:mealId` (`MealEditScreen.tsx`). A meal's pencil now calls `navigate()` instead of opening inline edit mode; `MealList.tsx` gained `date`/`focusMealId`/`onFocusedMealDone` props so the exact same edit UI/logic (drag reorder, item sheets, food picker, etc.) is reused unchanged, just constrained to one meal and auto-opened via lazy `useState` initializers (not a mount effect — avoids a setState-in-effect lint error). ~1,150 lines of edit-flow test coverage moved from `DailyEntryForm.test.tsx` to a new `MealEditScreen.test.tsx`; 5 test files needed `MemoryRouter` wrapping since `useNavigate()` now runs unconditionally wherever `MealList` mounts |
