## Tier 20 — Codebase-survey batch + two live-found issues (2026-07-19)
_`docs/potential-improvements.md`'s 2026-07-17 survey converted to issues, once its 3 already-covered items (bundle splitting #102, goal history #147, CSV export #125) were confirmed shipped and its "undo affordance" item was confirmed not worth filing (see that doc). Plus two issues found live while testing #156's fix, unrelated to the survey._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#168](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/168) | ✅ Done | Macros field has a different height/width than Calories | Reverted `self-start`, gave it the same full-width `h-12 items-center` treatment Weight's box uses |
| [#169](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/169) | ✅ Done | No way to cancel out of meal edit mode without saving or deleting | New `cancelEditMeal()` + ghost `X` button in the edit header, next to Delete |
| [#159](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/159) | ✅ Done | CI: run tests and lint before deploying, not just typecheck | Added `npm run lint` and `npx vitest run` steps to `deploy-pages.yml`, right after the existing type-check, before the build step |
| [#164](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/164) | ✅ Done | Add a "clear all my data" reset feature to Settings | New `ClearAllDataSection.tsx`, last card on the page, two-step confirm wiping all four IndexedDB tables then reloading |
| [#167](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/167) | ✅ Done | Extend the correlation-chart pattern to sleep and steps | New `domain/stats/sleepCorrelation.ts`/`stepsCorrelation.ts` + `SleepCorrelationView.tsx`/`StepsCorrelationView.tsx`, mirroring #116's exact day-pairing shape |
| [#170](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/170) | ✅ Done | Add Per-100g/Per-portion toggle to MealItemsSection's Settings nutrition editor | Added to both `MealItemRow`'s editor and #149's `AddMealItemForm`, mirroring #111's toggle from manual meal entry |
| [#172](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/172) | ✅ Done | Add search/filter by note text or mood to History | List view only (same scope as the existing date filter). New note-text search + mood filter reusing `EmotionPicker`'s click-again-to-clear semantics |
| [#163](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/163) | ✅ Done | Add offline support via a service worker | Added `vite-plugin-pwa` (Workbox `generateSW` strategy, `registerType: 'autoUpdate'`, `manifest: false` since `public/manifest.json` already exists) to `vite.config.ts` — precaches the entire built app shell (23 entries, ~2.3MB including the lazy `exceljs` chunk) so a cold load works with no live network fetch once the app has been opened at least once. `workbox.globIgnores: ['version.json']` keeps #115's update-check file a real always-fresh network fetch, never served from the SW cache. New `useOnlineStatus()` hook + `OfflineBanner.tsx` (quiet `navigator.onLine`-based indicator, mounted in `AppShell` next to `AppUpdateBanner`) — informational only, not a blocker, since the app is already fully IndexedDB-local. #102 (the other candidate explanation for slow cold-loads) turned out to already be fully resolved by its own root-cause fix (manifest path resolution), so no overlap to reconcile. Verified live: built + served the production bundle, loaded once online (letting the SW install), then went offline and reloaded — the full app shell rendered with no network requests |
| [#162](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/162) | ✅ Done | Add pagination/virtualization to History once data volume grows | Implemented as pagination (20 rows/page). `HistoryScreen.tsx` clamps the current page at render time (`Math.min(page, pageCount - 1)`) rather than tracking a filter-change reset effect — self-corrects whether the result set shrank from a filter or a delete |
| [#165](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/165) | ✅ Done | Do a full contrast re-audit across all mood/color-scheme combinations | Extended `src/theme.contrast.test.ts` (#11) rather than a one-off manual pass, so this stays a permanent, automated re-audit going forward — the original only checked hex-valued tokens (foreground/muted-foreground/card/primary/input), silently skipping anything in `oklch()`. Added a proper OKLCH→linear-sRGB conversion (Björn Ottosson's reference matrices) so `--destructive` and `--chart-1` are covered too, plus new checks for all 5 named chart series colors against `--background` (WCAG 1.4.11 non-text, 3:1). Also fixed a real pre-existing parser bug found along the way: an unanchored regex let `@custom-variant dark (&:is(.dark *));` (a `.dark` substring mid-line, unrelated to any real theme block) swallow the real bare `:root {}` rule into one garbled match — anchored the match to line-start instead. **Found 3 real, previously-undiscovered failures, all fixed**: (1) `--chart-calories: #c9a878` was ~2.1-2.2:1 against every light-mode background (needs 3.0) — every dark-mode block already sets `chart-calories` identical to `sand-foreground`, light mode just never got the same treatment; fixed by matching light mode to the same pattern. (2) `--chart-1` (Steps' only series) was `oklch(0.87 0 0)` — a near-white gray tuned for dark backgrounds, ~1.43:1 in light mode; split into a light-mode-specific override reusing the existing (otherwise-unused) `--chart-2` value, `oklch(0.556 0 0)`. (3) `--destructive` (intentionally mood-invariant) cleared 4.5:1 against Pond's own `--card` by only a hair (4.25:1) and failed outright against Tortoise's `--card` and both Tortoise's/Lagoon's `--background`; darkened from `oklch(0.577 ...)` to `oklch(0.52 ...)`, same hue/chroma, clearing every mood's worst case with margin. Dark-mode's own `--destructive` was already fine everywhere and is untouched. Side discovery, not acted on: `--sand-foreground` (used as the fix for #1) was itself completely unused by any component before this — dead CSS now put to actual use. Out of scope, not attempted: the `bg-destructive/10`/`text-destructive` alpha-blended button variant (`shared/ui/button.tsx`) needs a different, composite-over-background contrast calculation this token-pair test isn't built for |
| [#166](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/166) | ✅ Done | Do a dedicated keyboard-navigation audit of the denser screens | Audit only, no code change — a real keyboard-only walkthrough (Playwright driving actual `Tab`/`Arrow`/`Space`/`Escape` key presses against the running app, not just reading JSX) of `DailyEntryForm`'s add row, `MealItemEditorSheet` (name/mode-toggle/macros/emotion/Save), `FoodPickerDialog`, and the newly-built `RepeatMealDialog` (#202). No keyboard traps, no unreachable controls, tab order matches visual top-to-bottom order in every screen checked. Two things that looked like anomalies at first turned out to be correct, expected behavior: (1) native `<input type="date">`/`<input type="time">` each report as multiple consecutive tab stops (one per date/time segment) — standard browser behavior, not an app bug; (2) `MealItemEditorSheet`'s Save/"Save and add one more" briefly seemed to be missing from the tab cycle — they're `disabled` (and therefore correctly un-tabbable) until a valid kcal amount is typed, confirmed by re-running with an amount filled in, at which point both appeared in the right place. `ToggleGroup` (used by the per-100g/portion toggle and 8 more places on Settings) confirmed to support `ArrowLeft`/`ArrowRight` navigation within the group as a single tab stop, the correct ARIA radiogroup pattern — checked once, since every instance shares the same underlying component. No `releaseNotes.ts` entry — nothing user-facing changed |
| [#160](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/160) | ✅ Done | Decide whether CI should run on pull requests, not just push to main | Added `.github/workflows/ci.yml` — same typecheck/lint/test steps as `deploy-pages.yml`, triggered on `pull_request`, no build/deploy. No release note (#131/#144 precedent — zero end-user-facing behavior change) |
| [#161](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/161) | ✅ Done | Decide whether E2E/visual-regression testing is worth adding | Decided yes: `@playwright/test` + `playwright.config.ts` + `e2e/` (2 specs — daily-log add/edit via the pencil to `MealEditScreen`, and export → clear-all-data → re-import round-trip). Black-box UI interaction only, no direct IndexedDB seeding. Confirmed via `AskUserQuestion` that it should gate deploys, not stay local-only — wired into both `deploy-pages.yml` and `ci.yml` after the existing typecheck/lint/vitest steps. No release note (dev-tooling only, zero end-user-facing change, #131/#144/#160 precedent) |
| [#171](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/171) | ✅ Done | Consider a gentle, opt-in "haven't logged today" reminder | Implemented as an in-app-only banner (`useDailyReminderStore`, off by default) — no OS push notification, since the app has no backend to schedule one from and the existing no-pressure ethos (#14/#20/#29) ruled out anything alert-like. Shows on Today, same quiet-nudge style as the existing `showGoalRenewalReminder` (#38), only while viewing today and only when no entry exists yet |
| [#173](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/173) | ✅ Done | Empty space on the right returns in view mode after editing portion count and saving | Reproduces in both Safari and the web app (unlike #156, which was web-app-only), so likely a distinct cause despite the same visual symptom. **Checked the exact timing hypothesis for #197's fix applying here and it doesn't**: scripted the exact repro shape (open an existing item's editor sheet, change its portion quantity, click Save) and polled `body[data-scroll-locked]`/`margin-right` for 1.2s afterward — the attribute clears within ~130ms of Save (the sheet's own closing fade animation) and never reappears, in both the add-new-item and edit-existing-item flows. #197's fix only does anything while that attribute is present, so it can't explain a symptom reported as showing up "in view mode... after saving," once the dialog and its scroll-lock are already gone — root cause genuinely still unknown, **not** actually fixed by #197 despite closing alongside it. Closed on the reporter's own live confirmation (not observed since, across everything shipped today) rather than on code evidence — reopen with fresh repro details if it recurs |
| [#174](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/174) | ✅ Done | Prevent duplicate weekly goals, allow proper renewal, and allow deleting a wrong one | Scoped down from the issue's literal "disable Update until reached/window ends" (would've blocked fixing a same-day typo for up to a week — confirmed with the user, chose the narrower fix): `isDuplicateGoalSave()` no-op'd an exact same-day, same-target re-submission. Added `GoalRepository.deleteGoal()` + two-step confirm per row in `PastTargetsList`. **Superseded by #181**: the dedupe-only approach still left same-day *different*-value edits creating a second history row (reported live right after this shipped) — `isDuplicateGoalSave` was replaced by #181's edit-in-place model; the delete feature is unaffected |
| [#175](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/175) | ✅ Done | Navigation to Dashboard/History became very slow (10-20 seconds) | Originally closed here with no code change (reporter no longer observing it). Reopened later with ~6 years of imported data; real fix + on-device confirmation recorded in the later tier row for the same issue |
| [#176](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/176) | ✅ Done | Show local storage size used in Settings' Export section | `navigator.storage.estimate()` usage (not quota) shown as "~50 KB used on this device" under the Export card description; best-effort — shows nothing if the API is unavailable or rejects |
| [#177](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/177) | ✅ Done | Past-targets list shows "Target met" for a goal set moments ago | `goalWindowProgress()` now requires `MIN_WINDOW_DAYS_LOGGED` (2) days logged in the current window before assessing `targetMet`, and adds `metOnDate` (first day the running average crossed target) so `PastTargetsList` can show "Target met on Mar 12" instead of a bare "Target met" |
| [#178](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/178) | ✅ Done | Past-targets list shows weekly target as positive, should be negative (a loss) | Confirmed and fixed — `PastTargetsList.tsx` now negates `targetWeeklyLossKg` matching `GoalScreen.tsx`/`TodayScreen.tsx`'s own StatCards |
| [#179](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/179) | ✅ Done | Add search field and scrollable region to the meal-items list in Settings | `MealItemsSection.tsx` filters by name (same pattern as `FoodListSettingsScreen.tsx`), list capped at `max-h-96 overflow-y-auto` since it's embedded in a Settings Card, not its own page |
| [#180](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/180) | ✅ Done | Meal edit form shows large blank space on the right in Safari (regression of #156) | Filed as new issue rather than reopening #156 — reproduces in Safari browser but works in the installed app, the opposite surface from #156's confirmed root cause (`interactive-widget=resizes-content`, standalone-PWA-only). **Possibly fixed by #197's `body[data-scroll-locked]` margin-right fix** — the meal edit form opens the same Radix dialogs. Closed on the reporter's own live confirmation (not observed since) rather than on Chromium-only evidence — reopen with fresh details if it recurs |
| [#181](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/181) | ✅ Done | Edit the current week's goal in place instead of always creating a new history record | `formValuesToGoal(values, unit, existingGoal)` now reuses the same id/createdAt/weekStart while `existingGoal`'s window is still live (`isEditingLiveWindow`), only starting a fresh record once the window has ended. `isUnchangedGoalEdit()` (replaces `isDuplicateGoalSave`) live-disables the Update button for a true no-op. `pastGoals()` adds `approximateEndDate` for legacy (pre-#135) goals, derived from the superseding goal's `createdAt`. **Superseded by #182** below |
| [#182](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/182) | ✅ Done | Update button starts disabled on page load whenever the form is pre-filled with the unchanged value | Reported live right after #181 deployed: deleting Past-targets history doesn't touch the active goal, so the form still pre-filled with its unchanged value and Update was disabled by default with no clear affordance why. Fix: removed `isUnchangedGoalEdit`'s live-disable/notice entirely — a same-value resubmit is harmless (idempotent update to the same record) under #181's model, so there's nothing to guard against. User validated in the browser before commit |
| [#183](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/183) | ✅ Done | Support adding multiple foods to a meal in one flow (multi-add) | `FoodPickerDialog` now uses checkboxes (`selectedKeys: Set<string>`) instead of single-select — checked state survives a search-text change, and quantity/reaction fields only show while exactly one dish is checked (matches the old single-pick UX exactly, no regression there); a multi-pick defaults to 100g/no reaction, fine-tunable afterward via that item's own pencil. `onAdd` now takes the whole checked batch as one array call instead of one call per item. `MealItemEditorSheet` gained a second footer action, "Save and add one more" (only shown while adding a genuinely new item, not editing an existing one — `MealListItem` computes this via `entry.items`-membership). The bottom "add a new meal" row previously had no staging concept at all (every manual item became its own new meal record immediately) — added `addStagedItems`/`currentAddDraft()`/`stageAddItem()`, and a shared `draftsToItems()` helper (factored out of `saveEditMeal`) so the add row's final Save folds every staged dish into one grouped meal, same as an existing meal's `editItems` already did. Verified live: checkbox multi-add and "Save and add one more" both produce one meal card with multiple items, not separate cards. Confirmed via `AskUserQuestion` before implementing: checkbox-then-single-confirm (not add-per-check), "Save and add one more" keeps meal name/time but clears the item fields, and both mechanisms apply on `MealEditScreen` (#157) too since it reuses the same components |
| [#184](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/184) | ✅ Done | Custom-food name suggestions dropdown is expanded by default, should only open once typing starts | Root cause: `MealNoteAutocomplete.tsx`'s `onFocus={() => setIsOpen(true)}` opened the dropdown as soon as the Dish name field autofocused when the sheet opened, before any typing. Removed the `onFocus` handler entirely — only `onChange` (typing) opens it now. Verified live: the field shows a focus ring but no dropdown until the first keystroke |
| [#185](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/185) | ✅ Done | MealEditScreen doesn't scroll to top on navigation, lands mid-page | React Router doesn't reset scroll position on client-side navigation by default — added a `useEffect(() => window.scrollTo(0, 0), [pathname])` in `AppShell.tsx`, keyed on pathname only (not full location) so a search-param-only change doesn't jump the page |
| [#186](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/186) | ✅ Done | MealEditScreen intermittently shows blank space on the right, content and bottom nav shifted left | Regression from #157, reproduces even in browser emulator (unlike #156, which was real-device-only). Investigated live via a scripted Playwright reproduction across five angles (plain navigation, the delete-confirmation state with long Russian text, the Find-food dialog's Radix scroll-lock cleanup, a simulated stale horizontal scroll position, and a CPU/network-throttled sampling sweep across the whole route transition) — none reproduced any horizontal overflow. One of those five angles (the Find-food dialog's scroll-lock cleanup) was on the right track — **possibly fixed by #197's `body[data-scroll-locked]` margin-right fix**, found afterward by tracing the library's own source rather than black-box testing. Closed on the reporter's own live confirmation (not observed since) rather than on Chromium-only evidence — reopen with fresh details if it recurs |
| [#187](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/187) | ✅ Done | MealEditScreen always shows the meal-name placeholder for position 1 (Breakfast), regardless of the actual meal | Root cause: `MealList`'s `position={index + 1}` is always 1 for a single-element `[targetMeal]` array. Fixed via a new `focusMealPosition` prop — `MealEditScreen` now passes the meal's real position from the full day's `calorieEntries` (`targetMealIndex + 1`) |

---

## Tier 21 — Eleventh live-feedback wave (2026-07-20)

_Same pattern as Tiers 8–20: issues filed from continued live use, in one batch. Filed without pre-implementation investigation per the standing workflow — root causes below are "not yet investigated" unless noted otherwise. Ordered easiest → hardest per the usual default._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#193](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/193) | ✅ Done | Rename "Mood" (Настроение) to something clearer for the color-scheme/theme picker | `settings.moodLabel` display copy changed "Mood"/"Настроение" → "Theme"/"Тема" — couldn't reuse "Color scheme" since `colorSchemeLabel` already names the separate light/dark toggle just below it. Dictionary key name (`moodLabel`), the `Mood` type, and the 5 palette names (Pond/Dusk/Sage/Tortoise/Lagoon) are untouched — copy-only change |
| [#191](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/191) | ✅ Done | Storage-usage line in Settings only shows usage, not available space/quota | `ExportSection.tsx` now reads `estimate().quota` alongside `usage`; new `storageUsedOfQuotaLabel(used, quota)` ("~2.4 MB used of ~1.2 GB available on this device") replaces the plain usage-only line whenever quota is known, `storageUsedLabel` kept as a fallback for the (rare) case a browser returns usage but not quota. `formatBytes` gained a GB tier since quota values are commonly gigabyte-scale. Reverses #176's original decision to omit quota (reasoning then: it's usually a large, semi-arbitrary browser-computed ceiling, so "X of Y" would read as noise) — the real question asked here ("how much space is left, is there even a limit") is better answered by showing the actual number and letting the user judge it, than by the app deciding on their behalf it's not worth showing |
| [#196](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/196) | ✅ Done | History's input fields are a different size than Today's input fields | Confirmed: `shared/ui/input.tsx`'s base `Input` is `h-8`, and `HistoryScreen.tsx`'s From/To/search filter inputs never overrode it, unlike Today's Date field (`h-12` since #139). Added `h-12` to all three, matching the standalone-field height already used everywhere else |
| [#189](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/189) | ✅ Done | Day's note card doesn't resize properly around mood/edit icons when text wraps | Root cause: the read-only note display card (`DailyEntryForm.tsx`) was a fixed `h-12` — didn't grow for a note that wraps to multiple lines, so the mood icon/edit button (vertically centered against that too-short box) ended up overlapping the wrapped text. Changed to `min-h-12`, which keeps the same look for a short single-line note (the `icon-xl` edit button's own 44px plus centering still fills it out to 48px) but lets the card grow to fit a longer, wrapped one |
| [#195](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/195) | ✅ Done | Dashboard custom chart's series/mode selection isn't remembered across navigation | New `useCustomChartSelectionStore` (localStorage-persisted, same pattern as `weekStartStore`/`useUnitStore`) replaces `CustomChartView.tsx`'s three local `useState`s (`selectedNumeric`/`selectedBoolean`/`chartTypes`) — the toggled series and each series' line/bar/dots mode now survive navigating away from Dashboard and back |
| [#200](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/200) | ✅ Done | Editing a meal on a previous day navigates to today after saving | Confirmed root cause: `TodayScreen.tsx`'s viewed `date` (arrows, #138) was local `useState(todayIso)`, not reflected in the URL. Navigating to `MealEditScreen` (#157) and calling `navigate(-1)` to return does land back on `/` correctly, but `TodayScreen` fully remounts at that URL — local state doesn't survive a remount, so `date` always reinitialized to today regardless of which day was being viewed. Fixed by moving `date` into a `?date=` URL search param (`useSearchParams`, `replace: true` so browsing days doesn't spam the history stack) — `navigate(-1)` restores the exact prior URL including its query string, so the viewed day survives the round trip |
| [#192](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/192) | ✅ Done | Custom meal items list in Settings doesn't reliably capture its own scroll | Different root cause from #74 (that one was a `vh`-vs-`dvh` viewport-height issue inside a fixed-position dialog with the keyboard open — doesn't apply here, this list is a plain page section). Added `overscroll-y-contain` to `MealItemsSection.tsx`'s `max-h-96 overflow-y-auto` list (#179) — standard fix for scroll "chaining" up to the parent page once a touch-scroll gesture reaches the inner list's edge |
| [#198](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/198) | ✅ Done | July 16 calories look wrong/missing on Dashboard chart despite being logged | Not a data bug: `CustomChartView.tsx`'s "Compare data" chart normalizes each series to 0-100 within the *visible date range's own* min/max (`customChartPoints`), so whichever day happens to be that window's lowest value normalizes to exactly 0 — July 16's 918 kcal was genuinely the minimum of the visible range (only modestly below its neighbors), so its bar rendered at 0 height, reading as "nothing logged." Fixed by adding recharts' `minPointSize={3}` to the `<Bar>` element — its documented purpose is exactly this ("avoid a bar with a near-zero value not being visible"); the tooltip/legend still always read the real number, this only affects the rendered bar's minimum pixel height |
| [#199](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/199) | ✅ Done | Add a way to collapse the trailing "add a new meal" row once done for the day | Confirmed via `AskUserQuestion` before implementing (genuine design fork, flagged in the issue itself): collapsed state is a small "+ Add another meal" link (not fully hidden), and it resets automatically each new day rather than persisting. Implemented as plain `MealList.tsx` component state (`isAddRowCollapsed`, no store/localStorage needed) — Today already remounts `MealList` per date, so the "only means done for *today*" behavior falls out for free. A new "Done adding for today" ghost button at the bottom of the add-row card sets it; tapping the collapsed link's "+ Add another meal" unsets it. Hidden entirely (both states) in `MealEditScreen`'s single-meal focus mode, same as the add row itself already was |
| [#194](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/194) | ✅ Done | Action items on Settings page are visually inconsistent with Today page | Resolved the vagueness by inspection rather than asking, since a concrete cause was findable: `shared/ui/toggle-group.tsx`'s `ToggleGroupItem` has no explicit height, sizing purely from `py-1.5` + text (~32-36px) — well below the `h-12` (48px) standard #126/#133/#139 established for Today's own controls. Every `ToggleGroup` on `SettingsScreen.tsx` (units, week start, language, theme, light/dark, cycle/digestion/reminder toggles — 8 groups, 20 items) gained `h-12`, scoped to just this page's top-level preference toggles — deliberately not the shared component globally (would also affect deliberately-compact controls elsewhere, e.g. meal-editing's per-100g/portion toggle, per #126's own precedent) and not `MealItemsSection.tsx`/`FoodListSettingsScreen.tsx`'s nested toggles, which the reporter's screenshot didn't show |
| [#188](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/188) | ✅ Done | Bottom tab bar intermittently detaches, floats mid-page instead of staying anchored to the bottom | Didn't reproduce in Chromium (expected — the underlying mechanism is WebKit-specific), so reasoned about it logically per the reporter's own suggestion. The screenshot's symptom (bar mid-page, unrendered content both above and below it) matches #120's already-documented, already-"Partial" root cause exactly — that fix only hides the bar while a text input has DOM *focus*, but on iOS Safari the actual keyboard open/close is an *animated* viewport resize that can lag a focus/blur event by a couple hundred ms, during which the bar can still render at the wrong spot. New `useVisualViewportShrunk` hook widens the same mitigation with a second, independent signal — `window.visualViewport.height < window.innerHeight` — so the bar also stays hidden for as long as the viewport itself reads as shrunk, regardless of focus state. Same "not practically verifiable without a real device" caveat #120 already carries — this is a reasoned strengthening of that existing mitigation, not a confirmed fix |
| [#197](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/197) | ✅ Done | Layout breaks with blank space on the right: edit yesterday's meal, open a food, open a macro field | Traced to source and fixed, with a controlled (not just reasoned) verification. Radix Dialog's scroll-lock (`react-remove-scroll-bar`) computes a scrollbar-width "gap" as `window.innerWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth` and applies it as `margin-right: {gap}px !important` on `<body>` while any dialog is open — on iOS Safari, `window.innerWidth` is the exact value #156 already proved misreports under real keyboard-open conditions, while `clientWidth` doesn't shift the same way, so `gap` comes out as a large phantom number that renders as real blank space. New `html body[data-scroll-locked]` rule in `src/index.css` (higher specificity than the library's own `body[data-scroll-locked]` rule, so it reliably wins regardless of stylesheet order) zeroes just the `margin-right`/`padding-right` compensation, leaving the actual scroll lock (`overflow: hidden`) and the dialog's separate focus-trap mechanism untouched. **Verified live via a Playwright script** against the real running app (not just reasoning): opened a real `MealItemEditorSheet` dialog, faked `window.innerWidth` to reproduce #156's own measured delta (+90px), and confirmed with a proper control — without the override rule, `body` really does get a visible `margin-right: 90px`; with it, `0px`, scroll lock still intact. This proves the CSS mechanism is sound under a faithful simulation of the real broken condition; it does not prove the real device symptom is gone, since the actual WebKit `innerWidth` misreport still can't be triggered outside a real device (simulated here via `Object.defineProperty`, not naturally occurring) |
| [#190](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/190) | ✅ Done | Add a way to repeat/copy a previous meal instead of re-adding it by hand every day | Confirmed via `AskUserQuestion` before implementing (3 genuinely different scopes were on the table): a "Repeat yesterday's [meal]" quick action, over saved meal templates (biggest lift — new domain concept + Settings UI) or copy-from-History (more clicks to reach). Only offered for the *next* meal about to be added, when the previous day has a meal at that same 0-indexed position (#141's existing positional-identity convention, Breakfast = 1st meal etc. — not label text matching). Clicking clones only the objective food data (name/macros/`amountG`) with fresh ids into a new meal, one tap, no review step; day-specific journal details (time/note/per-item emotion) are deliberately *not* copied — cheap to re-add by hand if relevant, and copying them would presume things the app can't know. Touches the meal-item dictionary the same way every other add path does, skipping curated food names (#150). "Yesterday" is relative to whatever day `MealList` is showing (its own `date` prop), not literally today, so History's past-day editing gets the same feature correctly. Found and fixed a real bug along the way: the new direct-repository fetch caused unhandled promise rejections in `EntryRow.test.tsx`/`DayDetail.test.tsx` (no `fake-indexeddb` loaded there) — added a `.catch()`, same best-effort pattern as `usePastGoals`/`useMaxRecordedWeight` |

Also changed `collapseAddMealLabel` copy in **both** languages (not just Russian as originally asked) to the day-agnostic "Collapse"/"Свернуть" — the same button now also shows on a manually-expanded past day, where the English "Done adding for today" no longer made sense either. Broke ~41 existing tests across `MealList.test.tsx`/`DailyEntryForm.test.tsx`/`DayDetail.test.tsx` — their fixture dates (`2026-03-01` etc.) are genuinely in the past relative to the real clock, so the new correct past-day-collapsed behavior hid UI elements those tests expected open; fixed via `vi.useFakeTimers`/`vi.setSystemTime` frozen to each file's own fixture "today" rather than editing every literal date |

Every dish starts checked (matching the old all-or-nothing default); unchecking one before confirming excludes just that dish. `MealList.tsx`'s `repeatPreviousMeal()` (immediate commit) became `repeatSelectedItems(selected)`, called with only the dialog's confirmed subset |
| [#204](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/204) | ✅ Done | Search matches unrelated words that share a substring ("сыр" matches "сырой") | Word-boundary/prefix *exclusion* (initially proposed) turned out not to actually fix the report on closer inspection — "сырой"/"сырое" are complete standalone words, not mid-word substrings of "сыр", so word-boundary matching wouldn't exclude them either; they're genuinely different words that happen to share a 3-letter root. Reported this back with 3 real trade-off options rather than shipping something that wouldn't work; user chose reordering over exclusion — new shared `shared/lib/searchRank.ts` (`rankBySearchMatch`) reorders an already-filtered result set so exact/whole-word matches surface above mid-word ones, without excluding or changing which results appear. Scoped as one shared helper across all four search boxes (`FoodPickerDialog`, `MealNoteAutocomplete`, `MealItemsSection`, `FoodListSettingsScreen`), not just the reported food picker |
| [#203](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/203) | ✅ Done | Weekly goal shows "reached" despite weight going up 350g day-over-day | Implemented exactly per the design decided and paused earlier (2026-07-20): `goalWindowProgress()` fully rewritten to compare each day directly against `weekStart`'s own logged weight, no averaging on either side. `GoalWindowProgress` narrowed to `{ weekStart, weekEnd, targetMet, metOnDate }`; #177's `MIN_WINDOW_DAYS_LOGGED` gate removed as unnecessary under the new math. Full consumer sweep confirmed clean (only `targetMet`/`metOnDate`/`weekStart` were ever read). Reseeded stale "prior day + window days" fixtures in `GoalCelebrationModal.test.tsx`, `GoalScreen.test.tsx`, `goalHistory.test.ts`, `reachedGoalWindows.test.ts`, and `HistoryScreen.test.tsx` (one more file than the original blast-radius estimate, found by actually running each affected suite rather than trusting the estimate) — all used weight pairs that summed to "met" under the old averaging model but not the new day-over-day one. New test explicitly covers the reported regression case (a day-over-day weight *increase* from `weekStart` never reads as met) |
| [#166](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/166) | ✅ Done | Do a dedicated keyboard-navigation audit of the denser screens | Audit only, no code change — a real keyboard-only walkthrough (Playwright driving actual `Tab`/`Arrow`/`Space`/`Escape` key presses against the running app, not just reading JSX) of `DailyEntryForm`'s add row, `MealItemEditorSheet` (name/mode-toggle/macros/emotion/Save), `FoodPickerDialog`, and the newly-built `RepeatMealDialog` (#202). No keyboard traps, no unreachable controls, tab order matches visual top-to-bottom order in every screen checked. Two things that looked like anomalies at first turned out to be correct, expected behavior: (1) native `<input type="date">`/`<input type="time">` each report as multiple consecutive tab stops (one per date/time segment) — standard browser behavior, not an app bug; (2) `MealItemEditorSheet`'s Save/"Save and add one more" briefly seemed to be missing from the tab cycle — they're `disabled` (and therefore correctly un-tabbable) until a valid kcal amount is typed, confirmed by re-running with an amount filled in, at which point both appeared in the right place. `ToggleGroup` (used by the per-100g/portion toggle and 8 more places on Settings) confirmed to support `ArrowLeft`/`ArrowRight` navigation within the group as a single tab stop, the correct ARIA radiogroup pattern — checked once, since every instance shares the same underlying component. No `releaseNotes.ts` entry — nothing user-facing changed |

## Tier 23 — New reports (2026-07-20)

_Filed per explicit instruction ("add one more GitHub issue and address later once you get a chance"), not to be picked up until told to._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#205](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/205) | ✅ Done | "Reload" button on the update-available banner stopped working | Confirmed #163 (service worker, same day) as the cause. `useAppUpdateAvailable`'s fast `version.json` poll (5min) and the SW's own much slower background update check are on independent timers — a plain reload could just re-serve the still-active old worker's cache, banner reappearing right after, looking broken. Fix forces `registration.update()` right now (from the Reload click) and gives the resulting activation a bounded 5s window before actually reloading. Verified two ways: mocked unit tests for the exact timing contract, and a real production build + real Chromium service worker via Playwright (confirmed `registration.update()` doesn't throw against the real API and a real reload follows). Read the actual generated `dist/sw.js` before implementing — confirmed `registerType: 'autoUpdate'` bakes `skipWaiting()`/`clientsClaim()` directly into the worker's own top-level code, not behind a message listener, which ruled out an initially-planned `postMessage({ type: 'SKIP_WAITING' })` branch as dead code before it shipped |

_Filed per live report ("Log several issues"), not to be picked up until told to. Filed at the user's explicit request without any investigation between report and filing._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#207](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/207) | ✅ Done | Import fails with a generic error message | Root cause: `dailyEntries`/`mealItems` each have a *unique* secondary index (`date`/`name`) that their own `upsert()` deliberately enforces as a hard guarantee (pre-existing `index.test.ts` tests: "enforces one entry per date"/"enforces unique names") — `importAllData` was the one caller that didn't resolve to the existing record's own id first, so a re-imported backup (carrying its own ids, which essentially never match what this device generated for the same date/name since the backup was taken) hit a Dexie `ConstraintError` on almost any real re-import — caught, not thrown uncaught, hence "no console error." Fixed in `importAllData` itself (not the repository, to keep its own enforced-uniqueness contract and tests intact) — looks up existing entries/items by date/name first and carries the existing id over onto the imported record before upserting. Verified with a real browser + the user's actual backup file, seeding a same-date local collision first (the realistic scenario) — import succeeded, no duplicate, existing local record's id preserved with the backup's content |

Today gained a fourth `StatCard`, "Remaining calories," shown only once the active goal has the field set — the absolute difference between target and what's actually logged so far today (unlogged reads as 0 consumed, not unknown), with "kcal remaining"/"kcal over" as the unit text rather than a signed number, which would've read confusingly once negative. Verified visually against the real running app: set a target, logged a meal, watched the card update correctly |

Also drops the item from the current selection if it was checked. Verified live: seeded a personal item via a real add, opened Find food, deleted it, confirmed the row disappeared (the real app's store subscription re-renders the parent with a fresh `mealItems` prop) |

| [#211](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/211) | ✅ Done | Update-banner Reload button (#205) still unreliable on a real device | Recommended keeping both the button and pull-to-refresh rather than removing either — discovered `usePullToRefresh` (#118) already exists and already works in the standalone PWA context, contradicting `AppUpdateBanner.tsx`'s own stale doc comment (predating #118) claiming otherwise; the banner stays as the visible, no-gesture-required affordance, the gesture has no discoverable call-to-action of its own. Mitigated two ways: extracted `reloadForUpdate()` to `shared/lib/` so `usePullToRefresh`'s own reload (previously a plain, equally-stale-prone `window.location.reload()`) now gets the same update-aware logic instead of just the button; and `useAppUpdateAvailable` now also proactively calls `registration.update()` on its own 5-minute poll once an update is known to exist, spreading independent checks over time instead of only ever checking once at click-time. Verified both changed reload paths against a real service worker (production-config build, no console errors, real reload/navigation observed) |

Asked the user for repro specifics; once given (About page text), they retested and it worked fine — native selection handles + Copy/Translate menu, on both PWA and browser tab. Translated to Russian using the app's own existing name «Черепашка идёт к цели» rather than a literal word-for-word translation of "Turtle Steps," matching how the rest of the dictionary already handles this. _User supplied two external planning documents and asked for a relevance analysis: file GitHub issues for what's genuinely new and worth doing, and for anything that shouldn't be considered now, file it too but mark it Irrelevant/Paused with the reasoning. Both documents predate a lot of what the app has actually built — see the cross-reference note below before picking anything up from this tier._

**Cross-reference note:** significant overlap with already-built features was found and deliberately **not** filed as issues (would be pointless busywork): weekly summaries, the Dashboard itself, CSV/Excel/JSON export, backup/restore, calendar view, cycle tracking, steps-vs-weight and sleep-vs-weight correlation, dark-mode chart theming (#165), and the keyboard-nav audit (#166, already closed clean). The two source documents also directly **contradict each other** on water intake, exercise tracking, and steps/wearable sync — one lists them as things to add, the other explicitly lists them under "things I would remove" — neither side was picked silently. Separately, `improvement_suggestions.md`'s own "Things I would remove" section (protein tracking, mood tracking, meal logging, food database, steps) describes core, already-built, actively-used features — that section appears to predate or misunderstand the app's current state, and no "remove X" issues were filed from it.

Wired the same 7-day window into `WeightTrendChart.tsx` (matching the app's one existing rolling-average precedent, no window-size picker — 14/30-day were only ever "possibly" in the original request). Drawn as a dashed muted-gray line rather than a second solid `--chart-weight` line, since — unlike the calorie chart, where the average overlays a differently-colored Bar series — both weight series here are the same metric and would be hard to tell apart as two solid same-color lines. New `RecentAveragesCards.tsx` on the Dashboard renders a "Last 7 days" and "Last 30 days" `StatCard`, each showing average calories (kcal) with average protein as the description line; a window with no data in it is simply omitted rather than shown as a dash |
| [#216](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/216) | ✅ Done | Add calories-vs-weight and protein-vs-weight correlation | Resolved a genuine scope fork via `AskUserQuestion` first: the Dashboard already has a calories-vs-weight correlation (`CorrelationView.tsx`), but via a *weekly-average* mechanism, not the day-pair one (`sleepCorrelation.ts`/`stepsCorrelation.ts`) this issue's notes pointed at. User chose "protein only, leave calories as-is" — avoids a second, redundant calories-vs-weight section on the Dashboard. New `proteinCorrelation.ts` + `ProteinCorrelationView.tsx`, exact day-pair pattern as sleep/steps (protein total via `totalProtein`, paired with the *next* calendar day's weight change, `MIN_COMPARABLE_DAYS = 8`, median-split threshold rounded to the nearest 5g). Mounted on `DashboardScreen.tsx` right after `StepsCorrelationView` |
| [#217](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/217) | ✅ Done | Detect sparse/missing data, show "not enough data" instead of misleading charts | Extended the existing correlation-view "not enough data" gate pattern to the three trend charts: `WeightTrendChart`, `CalorieTrendChart`, `MacroTrendChart` now each define a local `MIN_TREND_DATA_POINTS = 3` and render a plain "not enough data" message instead of the chart when there are 1-2 logged points for that metric (0 points still renders nothing, unchanged). Below 3 points a straight line connecting just 1-2 far-apart points can visually read as a confident trend that isn't real — this is the exact case Recharts drew before (verified via screenshot: two points 19 days apart drew a full-width straight line). Deliberately scoped to just these three charts, not `CustomChartView` (its own per-series toggle/normalization logic is a separate concern) or the Weekly/Monthly/Recent-averages `StatCard`s (a single number per period isn't "misleading" the way a connected line spanning a sparse gap is — deeper per-period confidence framing is `#224`'s explicit territory, not this issue's). Threshold picked following the same reasoning as the existing `MIN_COMPARABLE_WEEKS`/`MIN_COMPARABLE_DAYS` gates elsewhere in `domain/stats/`, just for a raw trend line rather than a split-half comparison |

New `unusualEntryThresholds.ts`: `isUnusualWeightKg` (outside 35–250kg, entirely inside `dailyEntryFormSchema.ts`'s existing hard 20–400kg reject bounds) and `isUnusualDailyCalories` (day total over 6000 — nothing previously checked the *sum* across a day's meals, only each single item's own 10,000kcal cap). Weight: two-step "Save anyway"/"Fix it" confirm on the Save button, same shape the app's existing delete-confirms use. Calories: a quiet non-blocking inline note under the day's total, since a running sum across however many meals get added has no single "save" action to intercept. Verified visually against the real running app for both |

New "Export as Markdown" button in `ExportSection.tsx`, right after the CSV one. One deliberate difference from calories: clamped at 0 (`Math.max(0, target - logged)`) rather than reading "over" once met — more protein than planned isn't a "went over budget" concept the way a calorie ceiling is. User chose that scope over a wholesale "remove the Save-button pattern" redesign or a single-field pilot. New `mealDraftStorage.ts` persists the add-row's in-progress dish (name/kcal/macros/staged items/group note/time) to localStorage keyed by date, restored via a lazy `useState` initializer on mount and cleared once the meal group is actually saved (or the fields go back to blank by hand). Surfaced a real cross-test-pollution risk in the process — several existing test files reuse the same fixture date across many tests without clearing `localStorage`, which a persisted-by-default draft would otherwise leak between them; fixed in `DailyEntryForm.test.tsx`/`MealEditScreen.test.tsx`/`DayDetail.test.tsx` |

New `CompareRangesView.tsx` on the Dashboard: two independent date-range pickers (native `<input type="date">` pairs) plus a side-by-side `StatCard` pair and a signed weight-delta line. Defaults to this month vs. last month, matching the issue's own example, so there's something to look at before either picker is touched |
| [#223](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/223) | ✅ Done | Add a weekly heatmap view | Resolved the flagged ambiguity via `AskUserQuestion`: user chose a logging-consistency heatmap (GitHub-contribution-graph style) over a chosen-metric one. New `domain/stats/loggingConsistency.ts` (`loggingConsistencyWeeks`) scores each day 0-4 by how many core fields were logged (weight/meals/sleep/steps); new `LoggingConsistencyHeatmap.tsx` renders it as a vertical stack of week-rows (most-recent-first, capped at the last 12 weeks) rather than GitHub's horizontal year-view — a horizontal scroll is awkward on a phone, a 7-column grid isn't. Mounted on `DashboardScreen.tsx` right after `FoodReactionsView` |
| [#224](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/224) | ✅ Done | Add statistical confidence/outlier indicators to correlation views | Split into 3 pieces via `AskUserQuestion`: sample size (already shipped — every correlation view already shows its day/week count), a weak/moderate/strong plain-language strength label (shipped, see below), and outlier detection/exclude-from-calculations (**piece 3, shipped this round — see below**). **Strength label** (piece 2): new `domain/stats/correlationStrength.ts` — deterministic, non-statistical (this app's correlation views are median-split group-average comparisons, not a real Pearson coefficient, so there's no r-value to band): `classifyCorrelationStrength()` bands the absolute gap between the two compared groups' average weight change against fixed kg thresholds. Two threshold sets since the two kinds of delta aren't the same scale — `correlationInsight.ts` (calories) compares *weekly* deltas (0.15/0.35kg moderate/strong), the other four (sleep/steps/protein/late-meal) compare *day-pair* deltas (0.05/0.15kg). Rendered via a shared `CorrelationStrengthLabel.tsx`, reused identically across all 6 correlation views right under each one's existing day/week-count caveat line. **Outlier detection + exclusion** (piece 3): scope resolved via `AskUserQuestion` — applies to all 6 correlation views (weekly calories, late-meal, fasting-window, sleep, steps, protein), and a flagged point is tapped to toggle it out of that one view's own math, not a global exclusion. New `domain/stats/outlierDetection.ts` — deterministic Tukey's-fences rule (1.5×IQR beyond Q1/Q3), no distributional assumptions, consistent with `correlationStrength.ts`'s own fixed-threshold philosophy; `flagOutliers(points, getX, getY)` flags a point if *either* axis is an outlier (a vacation/illness week could show up as an unusual eating time, an unusual weight swing, or both). Each of the 6 domain modules gained a `*FromPoints(points)` variant (the existing median-split math, extracted so a view can filter out excluded points first) plus a stable per-point key (`date`, or `weekStart` for the weekly one — 4 of the 6 point interfaces gained this field, purely additive). New `stores/outlierExclusionStore.ts` (persisted, `Record<viewKey, Record<pointKey, true>>`) + shared `shared/hooks/useOutlierExclusion.ts` (flags + filters + toggle, reused by all 6 views the same way `CorrelationStrengthLabel` already is) + shared `features/dashboard/OutlierPointsList.tsx` (real tappable buttons below the chart, not a tiny SVG dot — recharts scatter points are an unreliable touch target, and this mirrors #275's own "explicit interactive element over tap-anywhere-on-the-chart" reasoning) + `outlierScatterShape.tsx` (a flagged point still renders in a distinct color on the chart itself for context; an excluded one renders dimmed). **Caught and fixed a real zustand bug while building this**: the exclusion-store selector's `?? {}` fallback created a new object reference every render, tripping React's `useSyncExternalStore`-based "Maximum update depth exceeded" infinite-loop guard the instant any view mounted — fixed with a module-level stable `EMPTY_EXCLUDED` constant instead. Full suite green. Confirmed by the user directly on-device |
| [#225](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/225) | ✅ Done | Track waist/hip circumference and body fat % | Three new independent optional `DailyEntry` fields (`waistCm`, `hipCm`, `bodyFatPercent`), same "purely additive, no export-schema version bump" pattern as sleep/steps. On Today's form, bundled under one "Body measurements" edit-toggle section (one label, one Save button, three sub-inputs) — same shape as the existing Sleep block's hours+deep-hours bundling — rather than three separate top-level fields, since a user updating one of these is likely updating the others together. Also wired into the Dashboard's "Compare your data" custom chart as three new toggleable series (reusing generic `--chart-2`/`--chart-3`/`--chart-4` CSS slots, same as Steps reusing `--chart-1`). No cm/inches unit conversion — stored and displayed in cm only, since the app has no established length-unit preference the way it does kg/lb for weight |
| [#226](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/226) | ✅ Done | Add a monthly summary card to the Dashboard | New `domain/stats/monthlySummaries.ts`, mirroring the existing `weeklySummaries.ts` shape but grouped by calendar month (no `weekStartsOn`-equivalent, a month has one unambiguous boundary). Deliberately no `targetMet` field — goals in this app are always weekly, no monthly-target concept exists to compare against, so summing weekly targets would invent a comparison rather than reuse one. New `MonthlySummaryCards.tsx`, same component shape as `WeeklySummaryCards.tsx`, rendered directly below it on the Dashboard. Verified visually against the real running app |
| [#227](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/227) | 🚫 Irrelevant | Logging streaks, habit score, achievement badges | Conflicts with the app's established no-gamification design (`TodayScreen.tsx`/`GoalCelebrationModal.tsx` both document this explicitly). Filed and closed for the record |
| [#228](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/228) | 🚫 Irrelevant | Long-term goal weight, pace prediction, completion date | Conflicts with #14's established decision to drop long-term goal-weight/date framing in favor of only ever showing the current week's target. Filed and closed for the record |
| [#229](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/229) | 🚫 Irrelevant | Apple Health / Google Fit / wearable device integrations | Conflicts with the app's local-only, no-cloud privacy model — no backend exists to integrate with a device/cloud API. Both source documents actually agree on this one. Filed and closed for the record |
| [#230](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/230) | 🚫 Irrelevant | Weekly AI-generated summary | Conflicts even with the *other* source document's own stated no-AI principle for this exact area; also no backend to call an AI API from. Filed and closed for the record |
| [#231](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/231) | 🚫 Irrelevant | Home screen widgets | Not achievable for a GitHub-Pages-hosted PWA without a native app wrapper. Filed and closed for the record |

## Tier 27 — Twelfth live-feedback wave (2026-07-21)

_New reports from the live app, filed for later triage — not yet implemented._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#232](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/232) | ✅ Done | Customizable sections on Today/Dashboard/Goal — dismiss via icon, restore via Settings toggle | Scope resolved via `AskUserQuestion`: reuse #245/#247's eye-icon toggle pattern (not the originally-described corner ×), applied to every remaining section across all three pages, not just the 3 examples circled. Dashboard: extended `dashboardChartVisibilityStore` to the 6 sections #247 hadn't reached (Food reactions, Logging consistency, Recent averages, Weekly/Monthly summary, Compare date ranges) — every Dashboard section now has the toggle. Today: new `sectionVisibilityStore` (separate store, different page/key set) covers 8 computed/insight sections (This week's target, vs. yesterday, vs. highest weight, remaining calories/protein, and the 3 banners) — deliberately excludes the raw input fields #237 already made toggleable. Goal: 3 sections (the weekly-target StatCard, the target-reached nudge, past targets) — GoalForm's own #244 read-only table stays untouched, it's core interactive UI, not a dismissible insight. Refactored `ChartTitleWithToggle` into a thin wrapper around a new store-agnostic `SectionTitleWithToggle` (`shared/ui/`) so Today/Goal could reuse it without duplicating the accessibility logic; extracted `VisibilityToggleButton` (just the eye icon, no title) so a `StatCard`-based section's toggle slots into the card's own label row instead of duplicating it in a separate title above — the first implementation attempt did exactly that and broke `getByText` uniqueness throughout the existing test suite. Confirmed by the user directly on-device |
| [#233](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/233) | ✅ Done | Discussion: additional stats to track, inspired by Zepp Life/Mi Fit body composition screen | Scope resolved via `AskUserQuestion` plus one explicit override of the initial recommendation — "add all the data from Mi Fit… make these elements also adjustable on settings page." **Manual daily entry** (new `DailyEntry.muscleMassKg/visceralFatRating/bodyWaterPercent/boneMassKg`, all optional, additive to the export schema, no version bump): bundled under one "Body composition" edit-toggle on `DailyEntryForm.tsx`, same shape as #225's Body measurements block, gated by a new `bodyComposition` key in `useTrackedFieldsStore` (so it's toggleable from Settings' "What to track" card like every other optional field). **Computed, not stored**: new `domain/stats/bodyComposition.ts` (`calculateBmi`, Mifflin-St Jeor `calculateBmr`) driven by a new local-only `useProfileStore` (height/age/sex — same non-exported-preference category as unit/theme, not part of backups) surfaced via a new Settings "Profile" card (`ProfileSection.tsx`, one Save button for all three fields). Today gained two new StatCards (BMI once height+today's weight exist; BMR once height+age+sex+weight all exist), individually hideable via #232's `sectionVisibilityStore` (`todayBmi`/`todayBmr`). Body score/Body type composite indices explicitly excluded per the user's own "skip these" answer. **Found and filed separately while verifying this live (#262)**: the fixed bottom tab bar can swallow the very first tap on a control right after a text input blurs, when that control sits near the bottom of the viewport — reproducible on the new Profile card's Age→Sex flow; root-caused and since fixed as its own issue. Confirmed by the user directly on-device |

Nav down to 5 items; `/about` still a real route. Confirmed by the user directly |

The gap was that it's a one-time dismissible dialog, easy to miss. Added a persistent banner (same shape as the #38 renewal reminder) as a complement, not a replacement. Confirmed by the user directly |
| [#236](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/236) | ✅ Done | Dashboard action icons too small for mobile touch targets (48px guidance) | Removed the custom size override entirely — the per-series chart-type `ToggleGroupItem`s now use the exact same default classes as the "Compare your data" series-picker buttons the user pointed at, guaranteeing visual parity rather than a guessed match. Confirmed by the user directly in the browser before closing |
| [#237](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/237) | ✅ Done | Unify all optional daily-tracking fields under one Settings "what to track" section | Scope resolved via `AskUserQuestion`: Sleep/Steps/Body measurements/Note/Mood (previously no opt-out) plus Cycle/Constipation tracking's existing separate opt-in toggles, all in one Settings card; toggling off only hides the Today input going forward, already-logged data stays visible everywhere else. New `useTrackedFieldsStore` (5 new fields); Cycle/Constipation keep their own existing stores (real persisted data, no migration risk) but render in the same unified card. Mood promoted from a sub-row inside Note's edit block to its own standalone, always-interactive field — it wasn't independently toggleable otherwise, since its only "save" path ran through Note's Save button. Confirmed by the user directly |
| [#238](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/238) | ✅ Done | Customize which chart series (raw vs. 7-day average) show on Dashboard trend charts | New persisted `useTrendChartSeriesStore`, independent per chart. Each chart's legend is now a clickable toggle per series; turning both off shows a "pick at least one" message, matching `CustomChartView`'s existing zero-selected-series pattern. **Regression caught live**: turning both off made the toggle buttons themselves disappear with no recovery path — fixed so the legend always renders, plus added a duplicate set of toggles to Settings as an always-reachable safety net. Confirmed by the user directly |
| [#244](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/244) | ✅ Done | Add a read-only current-goals table on the Goal page, with an edit icon to open the form | Surfaced live by #241 — once the form clears after save, there's no longer any visible display of the active daily calories/protein targets. `GoalForm.tsx` now toggles between a read-only `<table>` summary (all three settings, "Not set" for unset optional ones) + edit pencil, and the existing form — same display-then-edit shape `DailyEntryForm.tsx`'s Weight/Note fields use. Confirmed by the user directly on the deployed site |
| [#239](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/239) | ✅ Done | Move the selected date to the top of the Today page — currently jumps position per date | Moved the date row (arrows + input) to right under `PageHeader`, first thing after the title. Considered replacing the "Today" title with the date itself (the issue's own alternate phrasing) but didn't — every other screen's header is a fixed name via the same `PageHeader`; a bigger departure than needed to fix the reported jumping |
| [#240](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/240) | ✅ Done | Allow exporting a custom date range/period instead of full history | New "Export period" date-range picker (reused #222's `CompareRangesView` pattern), applies to Excel/CSV/Markdown only — the JSON backup deliberately stays complete regardless of the range, since a backup implies completeness. Confirmed by the user directly; follow-up question about whether backup should also support a range resolved — user confirmed it should stay always-complete, no code change needed |
| [#241](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/241) | ✅ Done | "Update this week's target" gives no visible feedback after a successful click | First attempt (checkmark, kept values) was explicitly corrected by the user — the fields themselves must clear. Root cause found via isolated repro: react-hook-form's `reset()` treats `undefined` as "leave the DOM value alone," needs an explicit `''`. Also fixed a real, separate bug found along the way: `shared/ui/input.tsx`'s `Input` wasn't `forwardRef`, so refs never reached the real input at all. Confirmed by the user directly |
| [#242](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/242) | ✅ Done | App-update Reload button shows no loading state, invites repeated clicks | `AppUpdateBanner.tsx` — `reloadForUpdate()`'s bounded wait (up to 5s) had no feedback in between. Button replaced entirely by a `RefreshCw`+spin "Reloading…" indicator once clicked, matching `PullToRefreshIndicator`'s existing spinner treatment; every path through `reloadForUpdate()` ends in a real reload, so nothing resets it back. Briefly reopened on a report that turned out to be the device on a stale cached build (mid-fix, showing "Version 216" when this shipped as 217) — re-confirmed fixed by the user directly after a genuinely fresh load |
| [#243](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/243) | ✅ Done | Day's mood icon next to the note still too small after #210's size bump | Root cause found by measuring the live DOM: the icon and the Edit pencil were already the same 16px, but (1) the icon was `text-muted-foreground` vs. the pencil's full `text-foreground`, and (2) the thin-outline mood glyphs need to render bigger than the solid pencil glyph to read as equally prominent. Fixed both; landed on `size-10` (40px) via a live side-by-side comparison the user picked from directly in their own browser |
| [#245](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/245) | ✅ Done | Add a per-chart show/hide toggle next to each Dashboard chart's title | New `useDashboardChartVisibilityStore` + shared `ChartTitleWithToggle` (title + eye-icon toggle), applied to the three trend charts (Weight/Calorie/Macro) — the ones with an obvious "chart title" to put a toggle next to. Broader Dashboard/Today/Goal section dismissal stays #232's separate scope. Applied the #238 lockout lesson directly — title+toggle always renders regardless of chart visibility state. Confirmed by the user directly on-device |
| [#246](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/246) | ✅ Done | Horizontal scrollbar on the History page, suspected 'kg' unit label | Confirmed real: at 320px width the Actions column's 3 icon buttons (non-wrappable) get clipped off screen by the table's `overflow-x-auto`, not a page-level leak. Moved the weight unit into the column header (`weightColumn` is now a function of the current unit) and tightened Actions to `icon-xs`/`gap-0.5` — the actual non-negotiable width floor, same class of fix as #136's date-format shortening and the macros-compact form. Confirmed by the user directly |
| [#247](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/247) | ✅ Done | Extend the #245 per-chart show/hide toggle to "Compare your data" and the correlation cards | Scope question resolved via `AskUserQuestion`: one toggle per correlation card (matches #245), not one for the whole group. `dashboardChartVisibilityStore` gained 6 new keys; `ChartTitleWithToggle` gained an `extraAction` slot so each correlation card's existing internal "Show chart" toggle can sit alongside the new whole-card eye toggle. Confirmed by the user directly |
| [#248](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/248) | ✅ Done | Add an option to include a brand name after the dish name | New `CalorieItem.brand?: string`, threaded through `MealItemEditorSheet` (new field right after Dish name) and every path in `MealList.tsx` (add row, existing-item edit, drafts, staging). Shown as `Name (Brand)` wherever a dish name already renders; also added as its own column in the Excel export's Meals sheet. CSV/Markdown exports don't break out individual meal items at all (day-level only), so no change needed there. Confirmed by the user directly |

## Tier 28 — Feature ideas from cross-project research (2026-07-21)

_Not from live-app feedback like Tiers 8–27 — filed after a comparative feature analysis against another nutrition-tracking codebase, cross-checked against this app's own `PROJECT_BRIEF.md`/`ARCHITECTURE.md`/this file first so nothing already-built got refiled. None of these are picked up yet._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#251](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/251) | ✅ Done | Add recipes — multi-ingredient, servings-based templates | Biggest capability gap found in the review — `MealItem` is a single dish with fixed per-100g macros; no entity for a multi-ingredient template (e.g. a pot of chili) logged by servings and edited in one place. **Domain** (`domain/recipe/`): `Recipe`/`RecipeIngredient` (structurally like `CalorieItem` minus `emotion`), `RecipeRepository` interface, `recipePerServing(recipe)`/`recipeServingsTotal(recipe, servingsEaten)` — combined ingredient totals ÷ yield, undefined-if-none-logged per-macro convention matching `calorieEntryTotals.ts`; both take `Pick<Recipe, 'ingredients' \| 'servings'>` so a live form preview can pass in-progress state without fake id/timestamps. **Persistence**: `IndexedDbRecipeRepository`, Dexie v10 (`recipes: 'id'` — no unique-name index, unlike `mealItems`, since recipes are always addressed by id, never looked up/deduplicated by name). **Store**: `useRecipeStore` (recipes/status/error, `loadRecipes`/`upsertRecipe`/`deleteRecipe`), simpler than `mealItemStore` since there's no name-collision merge logic to replicate. **Export/import**: `recipes` added to `exportBundleSchema.ts` as a purely-additive optional field, no version bump, same #113 precedent as `mealItems`/`foodOverrides`; import just upserts by id (no existing-id lookup needed, since there's no unique index to collide with). **UI** (`features/recipes/`, a new top-level feature folder — mirrors `export/`'s own "cohesive area, multiple usage sites" shape, since recipes need both a Settings-side management screen and a daily-log-side logging dialog): `RecipeEditorDialog.tsx` (build/edit — full-screen dialog, ingredients entered via the same per-100g-rate + quantity model everywhere else uses, `MealNoteAutocomplete` for ingredient-name suggestions restoring a `MealItem`'s last-logged rate via `ratesFromAbsolute`); `RecipesSettingsScreen.tsx` (list + edit/delete, new `/settings/recipes` route, reached via a new Settings card mirroring the existing Food list one); `LogRecipeDialog.tsx` ("log N servings of [recipe]", scales via `recipeServingsTotal`, hands back a `PickedFoodValues`-shaped result so `MealList.tsx` can feed it straight into its existing `addFoodEntry` rather than a second add-a-dish code path) — wired into the bottom add-row only, a deliberate v1 scope trim, same precedent #256 set for barcode scanning. `MealNoteAutocomplete`/`PickedFoodValues` are imported by direct file path, not through `daily-log`'s barrel — the barrel also re-exports `MealList.tsx`, which itself imports this feature's `LogRecipeDialog`, so going through the barrel would form an import cycle. Kept to the issue's own v1 scope: manual-entry only, no ingredient auto-suggestion beyond existing autocomplete, no "manual macros bypass" mode. Full suite green. Confirmed by the user directly on-device. **Follow-up filed as #303**: reported live — an ingredient's name/macros still have to be typed by hand even when the same food already exists in the saved-foods or curated list, unlike the daily log's own "Find food" search |
| [#252](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/252) | ✅ Done | Add daily fat and carb targets, alongside the existing calorie/protein ones | Exact sibling of #208/#220's existing independent-optional-target pattern — `Goal.dailyFatTargetG?`/`dailyCarbTargetG?` (purely additive, no version bump), two more `NumberInput`s on `GoalForm.tsx` with the same "leave blank to skip" hint, two new rows on the #244 read-only summary table ("Not set" when unset), and two new Today `StatCard`s ("Remaining fat"/"Remaining carbs", clamped at 0, each individually hideable via #232's `sectionVisibilityStore`). Confirmed by the user directly on-device |
| [#253](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/253) | ✅ Done | Copy/duplicate an entire previous day's meals, not just one | New `CopyDayMealsDialog.tsx` — whole-day sibling of `RepeatMealDialog` (#190/#202), same per-dish checkbox-then-confirm shape extended over every meal group in the source day. New "Copy yesterday's meals" button in `MealList.tsx`, independent of the per-meal "Repeat" button's position-matching. Only meal/food data copied, not weight/sleep/steps/note/mood/time/reactions. Confirmed by the user directly on-device |
| [#254](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/254) | ✅ Done | Add descriptive serving units (slice/cup/medium) for foods and meal items | Scope resolved via `AskUserQuestion`: lives in `FoodPickerDialog.tsx` only (not the unrelated manual-entry per-100g/per-portion toggle, which has no link to a specific curated food), seeded for ~10-15 obvious foods (egg sizes, bread slice, medium fruit/potato, cup of cooked rice/pasta) using standard USDA/FDA reference weights. New `FoodItem.servings?: FoodServing[]`; a `ToggleGroup` in the single-select footer swaps the grams field for a "how many" count once a descriptor's picked. Personal `MealItem` descriptors explicitly deferred, per the issue's own v1 scope. Confirmed by the user directly on-device |
| [#255](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/255) | ✅ Done | Flag a kcal/macro mismatch as a gentle sanity check on meal items | New `isInconsistentMacros(kcal, proteinG, fatG, carbsG)` in `unusualEntryThresholds.ts` (tolerance = larger of a flat 30kcal floor or 20% of entered kcal; only runs once all three macros are entered, to avoid flagging normal partial entries). Quiet, muted-not-red note in `MealItemEditorSheet.tsx` alongside the existing per-item/#260 preview lines. Confirmed by the user directly on-device |
| [#256](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/256) | ✅ Done | Add barcode scanning — local dictionary first, Open Food Facts fallback for unknown barcodes | New `MealItem.barcode?` field (sparse unique Dexie v9 index); scan checks the local dictionary first (fully offline), then — only if nothing local matches and the device is online (`useOnlineStatus`) — falls back to a client-side Open Food Facts fetch, parsed defensively. `lookupBarcode.ts` (pure, fully unit-tested) does the orchestration; `BarcodeScannerDialog.tsx` lazy-loads `@zxing/browser` (Safari has no native `BarcodeDetector`) only once opened and is itself lazily mounted by the caller. Wired into `MealList.tsx`'s bottom add-row (not the existing-meal-edit "Find food" — a deliberate v1 scope trim) via a new `EditItemDraft.barcode` (same "not a CalorieItem field, read at save time" shape as #279's `favorite`) and a new optional `MealItemEditorSheet.infoMessage` prop for the "no food found" note. The issue's own flagged open question (attach a scan to an existing item) was already resolved by its own checklist — explicitly marked "not required for v1" — so no fork remained to decide. **Found and fixed alongside, filed as #284**: #276's `favorite` field was never added to the export bundle schema at all, so it silently didn't survive backup/restore. Confirmed by the user directly on-device. **Follow-ups filed**: #288 (scanner missing from the edit-meal page), #289 (scanner missing from the Settings food-list add form) |

## Tier 29 — Fasting-window tracking (2026-07-21)

_Filed from a direct product discussion (not the Tier 28 cross-project code review) about whether time between meals correlates with weight change, and whether the popular "stop eating by a certain hour" claim holds up against the user's own data. Not picked up yet._

| # | Status | Issue | Notes |
|---|--------|-------|-------|
| [#257](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/257) | ✅ Done | Add fasting-window tracking — correlate time between meals with weight, plus an adjustable eating-cutoff comparison | Extends #116's existing "latest meal time vs. next-day weight" correlation, which uses a median split rather than an actual elapsed-duration metric. `domain/stats/fastingWindow.ts`: `fastingWindowPoints`/`fastingWindowCorrelation` compute the real elapsed hours between the previous day's last meal and the current day's first meal (median-split, `MIN_COMPARABLE_DAYS = 10`, stricter than #116's 8 since a point needs two consecutive days with a logged meal time). `FastingWindowCorrelationView.tsx` (scatter, same shape as `LateMealCorrelationView`); `fastingHours` also added as a toggleable series in the custom chart (#132) — precomputed via `fastingWindowPoints` and looked up by date, since it's the one series that depends on the previous day's data too, not a plain per-entry property. **Follow-up filed and implemented as #286**: the issue originally also shipped a second view, `fastingCutoffComparison` (a fixed, user-adjustable eating-cutoff Settings time), reported live as confusing/redundant once the window-correlation view above already answers the question with no configuration — removed entirely, see #286. Confirmed by the user directly on-device |
| [#286](https://github.com/ZhannaM85/turtle-steps-to-the-goal/issues/286) | ✅ Done | Remove the fasting eating-cutoff comparison (setting + view) added by #257 | Reported live right after #257 shipped — the Settings "eating cutoff time" input read as confusing with no clear payoff once the fasting-window correlation view (no setting needed) already answers "how long was my fast." Deleted `fastingCutoffStore.ts`, `FastingCutoffComparisonView.tsx` (+ test), `fastingCutoffComparison`/`FastingCutoffComparison` from `domain/stats/fastingWindow.ts` (+ tests), the Settings card, and the `dashboardChartVisibilityStore.fastingCutoffComparison` toggle key. `fastingWindowPoints`/`fastingWindowCorrelation`/`FastingWindowCorrelationView.tsx`/the `fastingHours` custom-chart series are all untouched — none of them depended on the cutoff setting. Confirmed by the user directly on-device |
